Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(rem)
Vol.4

Chapter 3845
Luo Nian felt somewhat disheartened by the giants' foolish reactions.
He suddenly recalled Elder Xue's words. The creatures born after the Great Tribulation in the Deep Space World were all very stupid. They were a race that couldn't be taught.
The Profound World had also suffered a Great Tribulation, so the situation was probably similar to the Deep Space World's. These giants' intelligence was pitifully low.
"Have you seen your master?"
Jiang Ziya asked.
Since these giants didn't understand, they could only eavesdrop bit by bit. Jiang Ziya was much more experienced in this aspect.
"No … … Master speaks in our minds!"
The giant replied.
Jiang Ziya nodded and asked, "Can everyone in your tribe hear him?"
"Everyone," the giant said.
"It's a will descending," Jiang Ziya muttered.
"No one in the Four Dimensional Worlds has such a powerful soul. Could it be a Degrader?"
Luo Nian asked suspiciously.
"It's very possible," Jiang Ziya said as he looked at the purple crystals on the backs of the giants. He then asked, "How did you get these purple crystals?"
If it really was a Degrader controlling these giants, then the purple crystals must have been given to them by the Degrader.
There was no way to tell if the Degrader could return to the Profound World, but he would definitely find a way to send the purple crystals out of the Chaotic World. From there, he might be able to find clues about the Chaotic World.
"The chief gave them to us. The chief bestowed us with great power!"
One of the giants said.
"Where is your chief?"
Jiang Ziya's voice became gentler.
At that moment, a loud voice rang out from afar. "No need to look. I'm right here!"
On top of a mountain not far away, a giant the size of a small hill appeared.
This giant's head was embedded with clusters of purple crystals. These purple crystals extended from his head all the way to his arms. Blood seeped out from the bottom of the crystals. They had just pierced into his body.
Then, one giant after another emerged from the mountain. In the blink of an eye, there were over a thousand of them.
Seeing these giants, Jiang Ziya became vigilant. He gripped the God Beating Whip tightly.
"That's great!"
Luo Nian smiled and stomped his foot on the mountain. His body formed a parabola as he smashed towards the chief.
Seeing Luo Nian make the first move, all of the Pang Hao Tribe members roared.
"Buzz!"
Every purple crystal on the patriarch's body began to glow.
An enormous amount of power stimulated every single meridian in his body. His eyes turned red, and a huge fist smashed towards Luo Nian.
Boom!
A sound that was neither crisp nor muffled rang out.
Luo Nian's charge was blocked by the patriarch, but the patriarch paid a terrible price. His thick arm began to crack from the middle, and the purple crystal embedded in his arm shot out like an arrow. More than half of his shoulder was torn apart.
Luo Nian took a step forward and grabbed the Patriarch's enormous head with one hand. "Where did you get these purple caltrop crystals?
I'll give you three breaths of time to answer, or die! "
Pang Hao's clansmen never thought that their prey would be so powerful …
The giants, including the patriarch, stood in place, not daring to move.
"I, I, Master gave it to me …" The Patriarch answered honestly.
"Where is Master?
Still three breaths of time. "Luo Nian asked again.
Just as the patriarch was about to speak, the purple crystal behind him began to flicker again.
Then, as if he'd gone mad, he ignored Luo Nian's threat and reached out with his remaining arm.
But the moment the patriarch attacked, Luo Nian's hand squeezed lightly, and the patriarch's head exploded.
"Roar!"
"Kill! Kill! Kill! Kill!"
"Eliminate the Beasts …"
The giants began to surround Luo Nian.
They weren't seeking revenge because of the patriarch's death. Rather, the purple crystals on their bodies were flickering with light, and they were using their violent power to cleanse their bodies and control their souls. The giants transformed into a group of flesh and blood puppets that attacked Luo Nian.
"Too slow. Too weak …."
Facing these opponents made Luo Nian feel like an adult beating up a child.
His body blurred, and he flitted through the giants like the wind. Every time he passed a giant, he would leave a fist mark on their head. The giants' heads rapidly deformed, disintegrated, and shattered ….
"Clang! Clang! Clang!"
The giants were like heavy wooden planks, smashing into the ground one after another.
In less than ten breaths of time, Luo Nian had already killed twenty or thirty giants.
But when Luo Nian glanced around, he saw that there were still many giants on the mountain ….
"There are too many …. Kill them all!"
Luo Nian raised the Skyring Staff and ordered.
"Whoosh …."
Almost a third of his power had been sucked away.
A sliver of lightning sprouted from the Skyring Staff, and then Luo Nian heard the crackle of lightning.
The entire mountain was covered in lightning. The lightning only lasted for an instant. After the lightning disappeared, all the giants maintained their original postures. Some were charging, some were climbing, and some were running. But their bodies were charred black, and their vitality had been extinguished.
Although he'd seen the power of the staff, Jiang Ziya couldn't help but be shocked.
After all, the higher the world, the lower the power level. Jiang Ziya's power had been severely weakened in the Profound Quantity World, but it was still terrifying in the Chaos World.
Luo Nian's attack was already so powerful in the Profound Quantity World. How much of a disturbance could it cause in the Chaos World?
Looking at the giant corpses, Luo Nian was slightly worried. "They've all been controlled. The enemy is too cunning."
Jiang Ziya wasn't discouraged. "Since they've targeted us, they'll definitely attack again …."
As he spoke, there was movement not far away. Luo Nian smiled faintly. "It seems that senior was right."
But when Luo Nian looked over, his face froze.
Another group of Pang Hao clansmen had appeared on the opposite mountain.
There were also over a thousand of them, and the leader was the same clan leader. Even the purple crystals on the clan leader's body were the same.
Jiang Ziya also realized something was wrong. "What! These giants are all clones? "
"Go!"
The clan leader waved his arm, and all the giants charged over from the opposite mountain.
Luo Nian frowned, and hesitantly raised the Skyring Staff.
"Kill them."
"Boom!"
The Skyring Staff sucked another third of Luo Nian's power.
Another wave of lightning fell ….
In the blink of an eye, this large group of giants was completely wiped out.
But after releasing the lightning twice, Luo Nian clearly felt a trace of weakness. More than two-thirds of his power had been used. If he released the lightning a third time, he wouldn't be able to use the Skyring Staff.
The power of the Skyring Staff depended on the user's power. Luo Nian had been strengthened twice by Levita, and was equivalent to a peak expert in deep space, but his power was limited.
At this moment, Luo Nian had a bad feeling. That hidden opponent seemed to know him and the Skyring Staff very well?

Chapter 3846
Just as Luo Nian was feeling slightly uneasy, he saw a ray of blue light flash past the left side of the mountain.
After this ray of blue light circled an area, the figures of giants appeared in that area.
There were still thousands of Pang Hao clansmen … "Senior Jiang Ziya, let's leave this place!"
Luo Nian said as he waved the Skyring Staff.
A formless wind pressure suddenly swept the two of them up into the sky, and they quickly left with the wind.
The Pang Hao clansmen saw Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya leave but did not chase. They could not catch up.
The patriarch looked at the sky and asked, "Master, they have already escaped."
"Bzzt bzzt bzzt …" Dots of five colored light appeared above the heads of the Pang Hao clansmen. These light waves were of different lengths and merged together to form a giant kun floating in the air.
If Luo Zheng saw this scene, he would recognize this as the Starry Sky Horned Kun he had seen in Soul City.
The Starry Sky Horned Kun was Ku Shu's mount. It was not a living being, but a ball of intelligent energy. As a member of the Four Spirit Gates, the Starry Sky Horned Kun should be the only existence that had escaped the great calamity.
For a long time, it had been circling outside the chaotic world. It could be seen in Three Clear Heaven.
The Starry Sky Horned Kun's voice quickly transmitted.
"Don't worry, they will not be able to escape … As long as I do not deactivate the 'absolute state of existence,' you will not truly die."
"I will continue to project you in front of them again and again until they are killed."
The Starry Sky Horned Kun had always been the holy beast of the Pang Hao Clan.
As a clan born after the great calamity, the Pang Hao Clan was able to grow to this day due to the mercy the Starry Sky Horned Kun had bestowed upon them.
Both the patriarch and the clansmen listened to the Starry Sky Horned Kun.
However, these Pang Hao clansmen didn't notice the change in the Starry Sky Horned Kun. The tip of its originally colorful horns was now mixed with a bit of black light, and the Starry Sky Horned Kun's lively eyes were now filled with confusion.
The Starry Sky Horned Kun was no longer the holy beast that had protected them for countless years … "Whoosh whoosh whoosh …" Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya flew a considerable distance in the air before slowly descending into a forest of giant trees.
The two stood on the crown of a giant tree. Both of them were slightly depressed, but Jiang Ziya was much calmer. Luo Nian's.
"We can't go back! What's the point of letting me get it?! "
As Luo Nian spoke, he waved the Skyring Staff around.
"Phew! Whoosh! Bang! Kacha … One of them smashed directly into the tree trunk. How could the thick tree trunk withstand Luo Nian's strength?
It snapped at the sound! Fortunately, the Tian Huan Staff was not an ordinary item. The seemingly crude tree stump was as hard as gold.
Under the setback, Luo Nian's nature erupted again.
Jiang Ziya remained silent.
He had no idea how to find the Chaos World.
He was also well aware of the consequences of not being able to return … While Luo Nian was still venting, another beam of blue light flashed across the treetops not far away.
Just like before, those damned Pang Hao clansmen appeared once again within the range of the blue light! "You just won't go away! All of you … are courting death! "
He had impulsively used the Skyring Staff without hesitation.
"Nian 'er!"
Jiang Ziya wanted to stop Luo Nian from using the Sky Ring Staff, but it was too late. Another third of the staff's power had been absorbed … The Sky Ring Staff would use a fixed power against a specific target.
As the power of the Sky Ring Staff flashed, another bolt of lightning enveloped the Pang Hao clansmen.
"Boom …" In the blink of an eye, the lives of these Pang Hao clansmen were ended.
The sound of thunder caused some birds to fly in all directions and cry out.
This time, Luo Nian felt weak after his power was sucked away.
His physical body's recovery ability was strong, but it could not withstand Luo Nian's squandering.
He had just killed this group of Pang Hao clansmen when a beam of blue light appeared on another treetops. These giants appeared again! "Don't waste time with them, let's go!"
Jiang Ziya said.
Luo Nian nodded. He forced himself to raise the Sky Ring Staff and used the wind pressure to pull him and Jiang Ziya up into the air.
Since the Pang Hao clansmen could follow them all the way here, he and Jiang Ziya were definitely being followed.
The other party chose to hide behind the Pang Hao clansmen not because they were afraid of Luo Nian, but because of the Sky Ring Staff in his hand … Once they saw that Luo Nian's power was depleted, they would probably attack and steal it.
"Phew … Senior Jiang Ziya, is there anything that can replenish energy?"
"Do you eat divine crystals?"
The Starry Expanse Realm had a higher standard. It was very inefficient to rely on materials from a lower level world to replenish energy.
After flying for a while, the two landed again.
However, the situation was exactly the same as before. Whenever they touched the ground, the beam of blue light would appear and shoot the giants over, forcing Luo Nian to fly up into the air again.
After three or four times, Luo Nian felt that not only did his power not recover, it was weakening! He would be finished sooner or later! After realizing this, Luo Nian suddenly made up his mind. "Bring us to the top of the Holy Pillar!"
Whoosh!
The higher the level of the world, the harder it was to move through space. Luo Nian was not confident. He did not know if the Sky Ring Staff could do greater teleportation, nor did he know if his physical strength could withstand it.
The Sky Ring Staff had almost sucked all of Luo Nian's physical strength dry. When his physical strength was insufficient, the Sky Ring Staff even started to suck the power of the soul! Luo Nian felt the world spin, and he almost fainted. Then, the two were wrapped in a layer of blue light and disappeared from the sky.
In the next moment … the two arrived at the top of the Holy Pillar.
Jiang Ziya was standing steadily on the top, while Luo Nian was powerless and directly fell onto the top.
"Senior, quick, quick, go tell my father …" In the present situation, they probably could not enter the chaos world. The only possibility was for Luo Zheng to come out.
Luo Nian had squeezed out the last of his power for this gamble.
Without another word, Jiang Ziya ran to the edge of the Holy Pillar. He placed his finger on the link of the sword and started to lightly pluck the string.
He had seen how Luo Nian operated it before. With his intelligence, he only needed a glance to understand.
On the Jade Platform.
Luo Zheng's group had been waiting.
The distance from the Holy Pillar to the chaos world was not far. Jiang Ziya could bring Luo Nian down from the Ji delete space at any time.
As time slowly passed, they became more and more impatient.
"Why haven't they descended?"
"Ji delete space can't descend?"
"Maybe they can't find the way back …" "Did something happen?"
Just as everyone was anxious, Hua Tianming's sword started to vibrate again. A new message came.
"I am Jiang Ziya. We can't find the chaos world and can't return!"

Chapter 3847
When everyone heard Hua Tianming's words, they were all stunned.
The Primal Chaos Realm was a huge world. Such a huge existence had to be fixed in one place. How could they not find it?
"You really can't find your way back?"
"Did you go the wrong way?"
"You can look carefully …"
Luo Zheng, Fuxi, and the others asked in confusion.
Nuwa interrupted them. "Jiang Ziya isn't an idiot. Luo Nian isn't stupid either. If they went the wrong way, they wouldn't have gone back to the Holy Pillar for help. The situation must be more complicated than we think. Listen to him!"
The people on the jade platform felt that Nuwa was right. They immediately quieted down and stared at Hua Tianming.
"We're being chased. The enemies are endless. Nian 'er can resist, but he's exhausted. I don't know how long he can last." Hua Tianming continued Jiang Ziya's words.
Luo Zheng, who had been calm, suddenly became excited. "Chased?
Who chased? "
After a while, Hua Tianming replied, "Some giants."
When the giants of the main world were mentioned, Luo Zheng's mind quickly thought of Bofan.
If there really were endless Pang Hao clansmen, it would be a huge crisis for Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya!
"What do we do?"
Luo Zheng stood in place and paced back and forth.
The Skyring Staff was outside, Jiang Ziya was outside, and Luo Nian was outside. Luo Zheng was as anxious as an ant on a hot pan.
After a few steps, Luo Zheng suddenly looked at Nuwa. "The Immortal Painting! Enter the Eternal God Court through the Immortal Painting! That's the fastest shortcut! "
Luo Zheng shouted that he wanted to enter the Immortal Painting, but no one on the jade platform responded.
In the end, the Primeval Lord of Heaven spoke. His remaining soul floated out of the Tool of Civilization. He sighed. "Luo Zheng, the Immortal Painting cannot be opened until the time is right. Moreover, you forgot that Ping Yi Heaven was also destroyed by the Degrader."
Ping Yi Heaven was destroyed. The Immortal Painting could not survive alone. It would collapse along with it.
Fuxi tilted his head, unable to find a solution. Nuwa pursed her lips.
Not long ago, Luo Nian had just completed a miraculous mission. Who would have thought that there would be a slip-up at the last moment, and the situation would take a turn for the worst. Most importantly, there was no solution …
They couldn't go to Jixu's space, much less the Xuan Liang World.
"Someone can send me to Jixue Space!"
Luo Zheng suddenly thought in his anxiety.
"Evil Deities?"
Nuwa also thought of this at the same time. She said dejectedly, "He might not be willing to help you. Also, don't forget that Jiang Ziya has shut down Ji Deleted space. Even if Evil Deities can control the truth of space, he still can't send people into Ji Deleted space …"
Ji Deleted Space was already under Jiang Ziya's control. Even the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods had no right to interfere.
"Hiss …"
Luo Zheng took a deep breath, the veins on his forehead bulging.
At this moment, Ku Shu suddenly said, "There is another way to go."
"What path?!"
Luo Zheng appeared in front of Ku Shu almost instantly.
"There are actually two paths in the Great Conjecture Institute's tunnel. One of them is for them to go home," Ku Shu answered.
As soon as he finished speaking, everyone looked over with a "why didn't you say so earlier" expression.
Ku Shu had his own considerations, "The problem is who to send over. Wen Dizhi and the Human Spirits of the Great Conjecture Institute, including me, can't return to the main world. Once they enter the main world, they will definitely die. Although you can enter the main world, it will be difficult for you to move around …"
Ku Shu knew that no one present had experienced the Scarlet Jade Ritual and could not understand the environment of the main world.
"I will go," Luo Zheng said calmly, "Maybe Nian 'er's life is not important, or Jiang Ziya's life is not important, but the Skyring Staff has to be taken back. Only I can take it back now!"
Jiang Ziya also mentioned that Luo Nian's stamina was exhausted …
Since the Skyring Staff had the same effect as the sword, it naturally needed a lot of energy. Only the Origin God Blood could satisfy it.
"You will be risking everything if you go," Ku Shu said.
"We have always been risking everything," Luo Zheng replied.
Of course, Ku Shu understood what Luo Zheng meant. They had no choice but to grit their teeth and charge forward.
"Then you go to the other side first …" Ku Shu said.
Luo Zheng originally planned to chant the True Meaning of Dao and enter the other side, but he looked up and said, "Lord Ku Shu, please wait …"
"Whoosh …"
Then Luo Zheng's figure disappeared. The next moment, he appeared in front of the Instrument of Civilization of the Tianzhi Civilization.
The Yu Sheng-tian was destroyed, and so was the Tianzhi Core. The Tianzhi Core was the most important thing of the Tianzhi Civilization. After it was destroyed, the Instrument of Civilization lost the necessity to carry it.
However, Luo Zheng did not give up on this Instrument of Civilization. It was a pity that the Tianzhi Civilization's unique calculation method was lost.
Therefore, he asked the Origin God Blood to help the Tianzhi Civilization restore the Tianzhi Core.
A huge cube appeared next to the Instrument of Civilization. This cube was constructed by the Origin God Blood using the Creation Quadrant ability. At this moment, there were three different colored lights constantly outlining the surface of the cube.
The Origin God Blood's ability was much stronger than the Tianzhi Civilization's. With its help and the Tianzhi Civilization's concept, the speed at which they fulfilled their wishes was surprisingly fast. The Tianzhi Civilization was confident that they could build an even stronger Tianzhi Core.
When the Tianzhi Civilization noticed Luo Zheng, a green figure quickly appeared.
"How is it?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"It's half done," the green figure replied.
"Can it be used?"
Luo Zheng asked again.
The green figure did not know what Luo Zheng wanted to do, but he still said, "It can be used, but it only has half of the calculation ability. The efficiency is very low …"
"I'm going to the main world now! After entering, I'll have to rely on you! "
"Ah?"
Before the green figure could react, Luo Zheng suddenly disappeared …
When he returned to the Emerald Platform, Luo Zheng had already sat cross-legged and nodded to Ku Shu, "Let's go to the other side!"
After entering the other side, Luo Zheng appeared inside the tunnel again.
Wen Dizhi saw Ku Shu and Luo Zheng enter the other side at the same time and felt a little strange. Ku Shu said, "He wants to go to the main world …"
"To the main world?"
Wen Dizhi was a little surprised, "What happened?"
After Ku Shu told him about the accident in the main world, Wen Dizhi fell into deep thought, "The end of the tunnel can indeed lead to the main world, but you have to go through the passage of the picture scroll. Before entering the main world, you have to face the executor."
When the word "executor" was mentioned, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
After Luo Zheng learned about the layout of the Snake Spirit King and the truth of the pyramid, he had already understood that the "executor" who kindly advised him was not a good person. He was so kind as to let him enter the pyramid and take out the sword!
Judging from this motive, the executor should be on the side of the Demoter.
Luo Zheng could go back and forth smoothly in the passage of the immortal picture scroll, but now it might not be the case. The executor would definitely obstruct him.

Chapter 3848
Luo Zheng didn't have any other choice so he could only choose this path.
"With the Origin God Blood helping me, I don't have to be afraid of it," Luo Zheng said.
Ku Shu didn't say anything. This was Luo Zheng's own choice.
Wen Dizhi saw that Luo Zheng was so insistent and didn't stop him. Of course he hoped that Luo Zheng could bring back the Skyring Staff.
The curse on the spirits of the Great Guessing Institute also came from the power of the deep space. If the Skyring Staff could be undone, they would be able to stop looking like ghosts.
"Since Sir Luo Zheng is so insistent, the Great Guessing Institute has no objections," Wen Dizhi nodded to the spirits behind him. The long-haired monsters opened a path.
At this time, light flashed beside Ku Shu. A golden figure slowly appeared. Ling Shuang had also returned to the path.
Ling Shuang had been in the state of descending and staying with Elder Ying in Li Mountain.
She heard that Luo Zheng planned to go to the Xuan Liang World alone and hurried over.
"Luo Zheng, I'll go with you!"
Ling Shuang said.
Seeing Ling Shuang recommend herself, Ku Shu's expression changed, "Shuang 'er, what are you doing?"
Ling Shuang glanced at Ku Shu.
Although she had a blood connection with Ku Shu, Ku Shu didn't know about Ling Shuang's experience in this life.
"I'm not doing anything," Ling Shuang shook her head, "Since I'm your daughter, I'm a spirit. Maybe I can walk freely in the Xuan Liang World!"
The people of the Great Guessing Institute had been speculating whether Ling Shuang had this ability.
Because there was no precedent, they couldn't be sure.
"If you're a spirit of the main world, you'll be killed by the rules of the main world. If you're not, you'll only be a burden to Luo Zheng. So you can't go!"
Ku Shu stepped in front of Ling Shuang.
"Ling Shuang," Luo Zheng frowned.
Ling Shuang was so stubborn that even Elder Ying couldn't stop her, let alone Ku Shu and the people of the Great Guessing Institute.
However, as soon as she took two steps, Wen Dizhi stretched out his hand and revealed an oval-shaped gemstone in his furry palm. The gemstone flashed, and Ling Shuang felt dizzy and was about to fall.
Ku Shu stepped forward to support her and said to Luo Zheng, "Go!"
Under the guidance of Wen Dizhi, Luo Zheng walked all the way to the end of the tunnel, where there was a rusty iron door. Of course, this iron door was not made of ordinary iron. It could withstand the erosion of trillions of years.
When they walked to the door, Wen Dizhi said, "This passage was built after we were trapped in the Chaotic World. Although it still relies on the Immortal Picture Scroll as a passage, it is actually a shortcut. If you go in and pass it quickly, you may be able to avoid contact with the executor …" Wen Dizhi was the one who speculated that the executor might be on the side of the degenerate. After combining the motives before and after judgment, Luo Zheng and Ku Shu were also inclined to Wen Dizhi's conclusion.
Whether or not the author really had such an attitude was still uncertain, but avoiding the author was still the safest option.
Luo Zheng nodded, opened the iron door and stepped into the dark void.
The other side of the iron door was not void, but more like an unbreakable wall. As soon as Luo Zheng entered, he was crushed against the wall by an irresistible force and became flattened.
Because he had once entered the Immortal Picture Scroll, Luo Zheng quickly adapted to it.
"A straight path!"
As Wen Dizhi said, although this path was the passage of the Immortal Picture Scroll, he did not need to pass the layers of tests in the Immortal Picture Scroll.
Luo Zheng did not stop. Like a crab fleeing for its life, he ran along this path.
After dozens of breaths, Luo Zheng had reached the end of this passage.
When he continued to move forward, a new passage appeared again.
Luo Zheng was confused for a moment, and then realized that this was the second page of the Immortal Picture Scroll.
There were three pages in the Immortal Picture Scroll, and he had to pass the last page.
He kept moving forward at a high speed, and soon passed the second page. On the third page, the last passage appeared in front of Luo Zheng again. Through this passage, he could reach the outside of the Eternal Divine Court! Continue … When he had walked more than half the distance on the third page, Luo Zheng saw a painted door at the end of the plane.
This painted door was open, and he could just get out! Although Luo Zheng was now in a flattened form, his heart was still beating nervously, and he prayed in his heart, Let me pass, let me pass … He was getting closer and closer to the door, and was only two or three steps away. He could get in in the blink of an eye. But at this moment, a thick ink stain appeared on the Immortal Picture Scroll.
This ink stain was fiercely smeared on the door.
The road was blocked.
Luo Zheng's forward steps also stopped. Even in his flat form, one could see that his face was gloomy.
Last time he met the author, he could not confirm the author's purpose, but this time it was different. There was no need to communicate, ask, or question. He could see the author's purpose just by looking at his behavior.
"Swish, swish, swish, swish!"
Four thick ink stains appeared around Luo Zheng one by one. These four ink stains were like a prison, surrounding Luo Zheng.
"Author," Luo Zheng said.
In this form, Luo Zheng could not see the author, but he knew that the author was looking down at him from above the picture scroll.
"You didn't let me down. You finally opened the Altar of Earth. You are better than I thought …" A huge crying face above the picture scroll said with a smile.
"In that case, why did you stop me?" Luo Zheng asked.
The author's crying face turned into a smiling face, but he said in a mournful voice, "Your task has been completed. Don't make trouble. You can either go back to the Chaotic World, or … stay here."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was a little angry. He raised his voice and said, "Do you think the Degrader will do as you wish?
When it created you, it left a foreshadowing for you to think that the two-grid world is perfect. But it has told such a lie many times! It lied to the Four Spirit Gates that the three-grid world is good. It lied to the Dan Clan that the four-grid world is good. It also lied to the Shengming that the five-grid world is good! "
"I don't care so much. My long-cherished wish is to expand the entire World of the Giant Wall, not just limited to the immortal picture scroll," the author said.
In fact, the "immortal picture scroll" was not the real World of the Giant Wall. Because the creatures in the World of the Giant Wall, as a picture in the world, could never see their full appearance. The immortal picture scroll was a false simulation, and the author wanted to turn this simulation into reality.
"Even if the World of the Giant Wall is realized, it will not stop! It will still reduce the World of the Giant Wall to a one-grid world. You can't escape the fate of destruction in the end! "
Luo Zheng said angrily.
This was the result that could be expected. The author, who was used to the vicissitudes of life, was so stupid.
"Even if it eventually becomes a one-grid world, it will stay in the World of the Giant Wall. Even if it stays for a few years or decades, I will be satisfied. Not to mention that the three-grid world has existed for a long time. The World of the Giant Wall should exist for a longer time. There will be more two-grid creatures in it, and the World of the Giant Wall will be more prosperous!"
The author's voice had a trace of fanaticism.

Chapter 3849
From the birth of the executor, it regarded itself as the God of the Wall World, and it had a strong obsession with the Wall World.
If the Descendant turned the whole world into a Wall World, it would be as he wished.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to persuade the executor.
However, its muttering caused Luo Zheng's heart to turn cold.
"Even if the Descendant really turns into a Wall World, your end won't be good. If you don't believe me, you can look at the end of 'Sin'!"
Luo Zheng said.
"I don't care," the Descendant said with some fanaticism, "I just want to know that the Wall World can appear! So you can't pass, you can only … die here! "
It knew that Luo Zheng had the Origin God's blood, so it didn't dare to take it lightly. The best way was to completely kill Luo Zheng in the immortal picture scroll.
A huge ink pen floated above the immortal picture scroll, drawing a blade-like handwriting on the snow-white picture scroll.
"Swish!"
The ink mark swept past.
Luo Zheng felt a strong force cutting through his waist.

"Everyone, please go to the second floor from the back stairs," the voice of Evil Deities came from all directions.
Han Ge and Tongtian walked in front while the others followed closely behind.
The Evil God's main body stood at the center of the hall on the second floor. However, the gazes of the Holy Soul and Immortal Realm experts didn't linger on him. They were all shocked by the scene on the ceiling.
There were diamond-shaped crystals inlaid on the ceiling. There were also various kinds of energy flowing within the crystals. The aura of these energies was very familiar to them. It was the power of bloodlines!
"Heavens! These are all bloodline powers! "
"One, two, three … A total of three hundred and seventy-three types?"
"Are all the bloodline powers here?"
The Saint Soul Realm powerhouses did not have the opportunity to come into contact with bloodline powers. Even the Immortal Realm powerhouses could only obtain one type of bloodline from the Immortal Painting. They could not be compared to any of the bloodline powers on the ceiling.
They were like a group of beggars who had suddenly barged into a cave full of gold and silver. Their eyes were dazzled.
Han Ge and Tongtian were still fine as they had already known about it. However, when they saw the complete bloodline powers neatly arranged on the ceiling, they were still very shocked.
"You can choose one type of bloodline power. I will bestow it to you …" Evil Deities said.
Evil Deities' words echoed in their ears like the sound of nature.
After Evil Deities took control of the Mother World, the powerhouses of the various races were dissatisfied. However, in the face of absolute power, they could only suppress their dissatisfaction in their hearts.
Now that Evil Deities was so generous, the dissatisfaction in their hearts instantly disappeared.
Before the Saint Soul Realm and Immortal Realm powerhouses could express their gratitude, Evil Deities said to Tongtian and Han Ge, "Both of you, please follow me to the third floor."
Han Ge and Tongtian looked at each other.
They already had an even more unbelievable guess in their hearts.
Without saying a word, they stepped up the stairs and entered the hall on the third floor.
At the top of the hall, there were many rings inlaid. However, these rings were empty. There was no energy flowing within them.
Han Ge's eyes quickly scanned and counted the number of rings. 140 …
At this moment, Evil Deities raised his head slightly.
"Bzzz …"
One ring after another lit up.
All kinds of extreme energy filled the rings!
Tongtian's mouth was wide open as he felt the extreme energy in the rings in a daze.
"This, this …"
"This is all the energy in the deep space?"
The two of them had seen Evil Deities restore the extreme energy in the deep space. Although they were already mentally prepared, when they saw so much extreme energy being displayed, they still found it hard to suppress their excitement.
"I can also teach you these extreme energies, but …" Evil Deities frowned and said, "These extreme energies consume a lot of energy. Even I can't maintain them forever."

Chapter 3858
All matter was energy, and all energy was made of matter.
The third grid, Lower Extreme Realm, was made of 3,000 Divine Path energies. Water, Fire, Wind, Grass, Wood, Stone, and Sand were all formed from the most basic Divine Path energies.
Evil Deities could be supplied with the energy of the Inner World.
Everything in the lower realm of the four squares was formed by the power of the bloodline.
Fusing bloodlines was essentially a form of transformation. After the Immortal Realm inherited the power of bloodlines, they relied on their own bloodlines to supply it.
However, Evil Deities had not undergone transformation. He was not an Immortal Realm powerhouse, and he relied entirely on the energy of the Mother World to restore himself.
Restoring the power of bloodlines was still fine. The Mother World was so vast now, and the energy it contained was enough for Evil Deities to squander for a long time.
However, the energy consumption of the Extreme Realm in the Deep Space was terrifying! Even if he relied on the Mother World, he did not dare to squander it casually. Therefore, when he showed it to Tongtian and Han Ge, he lit up the 140 types of Extreme Realm energy in the ring. At other times, he had to use it sparingly.

Han Ge had seen the power of Extreme Realm energy. It basically beat the power of bloodlines in all aspects.
"It's up to you." Evil Deities was not surprised by their choice.
Tongtian looked at the end of the hall. There was a flight of stairs, which obviously led to a higher floor.

Tongtian asked.
Compared to Han Ge, Tongtian knew Evil Deities better.

"The third floor is not open for now." Evil Deities waved his hand with a hint of arrogance. He did not say whether or not it was open.

Tongtian asked.

A mother world could not resist the Downtrodden. Evil Deities made the mother world and Luo Zheng's Land of Beginnings act as if they wanted to join forces to fight against the Downtrodden.

This Sentinel Evil Deities was just predicting the actions of Evil Deities.

Suddenly, two "moons" rose from the dark mountain peak. They were like crescent moons hanging in the sky.

After the battle with Yu Qingtian, Black Light no longer had any scruples and directly called itself a Downtrodden.
Since then, Sin knew that its fantasy was shattered. It would never be able to return to the main world, to its former home.

"Both of them?
Where are they going? "
The Serpent King asked with concern.

Chapter 3859

Ten days later.
The Land of Extinction began to slow down when it was a million miles away from the inner wall of chaos. This was a slow process.

Was it a force that opposed his father?
Her heart, which had been imprisoned for over a thousand chaos epochs, suddenly began to throb.
All these years, she had never dared to hope because she had long lost her qualifications.
However, whenever there was a slim chance, she couldn't help but fantasize.
The colossal creature came closer and closer, finally stopping within a hundred thousand miles.

After pausing for half an hour, Luo Zheng's figure appeared outside the Land of Origin.
He stretched out his finger and gently pointed in the air. Ripples appeared in the space in front of him. As soon as the ripples appeared, they turned the space upside down, folding the space within a hundred thousand miles. Then, they calmed down quietly.
Just like that, Luo Zheng was already a hundred thousand li away.
"Ka Ka Ka Ka Ka …" Those heads were still crazily hitting the barrier.

For those heads, who only knew how to devour, Luo Zheng was a top-grade delicacy! They gave up on Yu Zhi and turned their heads toward Luo Zheng.

"Human?"
Yu Zhi's face showed a strange expression.

These shadows all reached out and gently knocked on the foreheads of the heads.
"Tap, tap, tap …" Then Luo Zheng passed through these heads like a ghost and slowly flew toward Yu Zhi.
"Those heads are not dead yet, you are in danger …" Yu Zhi reminded.
Those heads turned around, opened their mouths wide and bit at Luo Zheng's head.

After Luo Zheng walked in, a light blue enchantment floated in front of Yu Zhi.
This enchantment could block the attacks of the heads, and its defensive power was naturally extraordinary.
But after Luo Zheng walked in, he stretched out a finger toward the enchantment, and then a purple-black energy circle appeared on Luo Zheng's wrist, and his entire arm turned into an energy arm of extreme destruction.
"Swish …" Luo Zheng gently stroked, and the enchantment shattered like a fragile thin ice.

"Daughter?
Husband? "
Hearing this, Yu Zhi's head became very stiff.

He stretched out his hand and pressed lightly in the air.
Violent fluctuations appeared in the space again, and after the two spaces folded, Ling Shuang and Ku Shu floated next to Luo Zheng.
"Mother …" Ling Shuang called.
The moment she saw this woman, a sense of intimacy emerged from her bones, which came from the connection of her bloodline.
"Yu Zhi …" Ku Shu called out affectionately.
When she was first sent into the Human Spirit Sect, she didn't have a name, and the name Yu Zhi was given by Ku Shu.
In the long years since, Ku Shu had never thought that he would see her again.
Even the King of the Human Spirit Sect's emotions fluctuated.
"Wuwuwu …" Yu Zhi was dazed for a moment, and then directly sobbed. Ku Shu hurriedly stepped forward and held her face, "I've made you suffer …" Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was gratified, but also felt inconvenient, and his figure moved far away, leaving the family of three to reunite.
Half an hour later, Ku Shu waved at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng approached again, and the way Yu Zhi looked at Luo Zheng was different.
She already knew Luo Zheng's identity, and also understood Ling Shuang's feelings through Ku Shu. If this marriage was successful, the person in front of her would be her son-in-law.
"The inner wall of the chaos is very strong, please bear with it!"
Luo Zheng warned, and put one hand on the wall in front of Yu Zhi.
From his hand, strands of purple-black energy flowed, still in the Extreme State of Destruction.
This energy was all-pervasive, and directly drilled into the inner wall of the chaos. After the inner wall was dyed purple-black, this layer of the inner wall had already decayed, and it only took a little force to dig out the inner wall piece by piece.

Chapter 3860
After Luo Zheng released Yuzhi from the wall, the ferocious bead suddenly appeared above her head.

Yuzhi looked up at the bead and became very agitated.

Ling Shuang shouted and wanted to rush over, but she was stopped by Ku Shu. "Don't go. Your mother won't die."

Six days later.
The "Sentinel" Evil Deities was lying on a chain. The chain swung back and forth like a hammock.
His duty was to keep watch for the mother world. He used the "Phantom Vision" to cover a range of 20,000 miles at any time and place.

Two million miles …

Ten days later …
Another Sealing Stone descended.
Previously, a wave of Sealing Stones had descended.
At that time, everyone thought that the end was coming, but suddenly there was no movement. They didn't expect another Sealing Stone to descend in the mother world. This time, everyone became nervous!
Not long after this Sealing Stone descended, the entire Chaotic World began to shake.
They immediately understood why the Land of Extinction had been biding its time!
This was the last grain of sand in the hourglass.
The Downtrodden was waiting for the end to descend!
At the last moment of the past Chaos Epochs, the end meant the end of the world.
All matter in the Chaotic World was reshuffled. In each Chaos Epoch, only six beings were chosen as Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. From then on, a new Chaos Epoch would begin.
No one had ever fought against the End. All beings in the Chaos World saw fighting against the End as their ultimate goal.
In this Chaos Epoch, they had the confidence to fight against the End. However, the situation had changed completely.
Literature Hall

Chapter 3861
Every time the End came, there would always be some creatures escaping to the Nirvana Realm, so people were not unfamiliar with the End.
As long as one reached the Nirvana Realm, it was easy to find descriptions and records of the End.
It was not a secret, but a disaster that no civilization could successfully resist.
In most of the descriptions of the End, it was recorded that the End was a huge needle that directly pierced through the Land of Origin, and then smashed it into pieces, destroying everything …
This was the most intuitive description, but it was actually not accurate.
The appearance of the "needle" was actually the last stage of the End. In fact, the End was actually a series of processes, consisting of several stages.
When the last seal stone descended, the entire Chaotic World would shake irregularly, and at the same time, there would be a loud noise.
The loud noise came from the collision between the Chaotic World and the Main World, and the sound was like the most powerful thunder roar, which was also the first disaster of the End. The thunder-like loud noise turned into destructive sound waves, and would spread throughout the Chaotic World within three days.
Those weak creatures living in the Chaotic World would be directly shaken into blood foam by the sound waves, and the buildings built around the bottom of the Chaotic World would also collapse, turning into clouds of white mist.
On the other hand, the living beings in the Land of Origin were much luckier because they had a natural barrier.
"Rumble rumble rumble …"
The shockingly destructive sound waves spread rapidly in the Chaotic World.
Previously, the Snake Spirit King had already activated the descent, and the corners of the Chaotic World were swallowed up by the heads. The creatures living in the Chaotic World were already buried in the mouths of the heads, and they were spared from the fate of being shaken into blood foam.
After fourteen hours, the sound waves spread toward the Mother World.
The sentinel Evil Deities saw some faint distortions in the space in the distance, and he tilted his head and stroked his hair expressionlessly. The next moment, his ears exploded, and two blood arrows shot out from his ears.
In fact, with his cultivation, he could easily deal with it with a little protection, but he deliberately experienced the feeling of the sound waves.
Because he was suddenly deafened, the sentinel Evil Deities had to repair his eardrums, so that he could "hear" clearly.
The thick outer wall of the Mother World offset most of the energy of the sound waves, and the sound waves were greatly reduced by the time they reached the inside.
But even if the power was greatly reduced, the roar was still far beyond the sound of ordinary thunder.
"Rumble …"
At first, the foreign races in the Mother World didn't know what was going on. They only thought that the weather was abnormal, or that a powerhouse had released the power of thunder.
But even after four hours, the thunderous rumbling still hadn't subsided. The Outsiders couldn't even use language to converse, and could only communicate with their divine sense. Some of the Outsiders couldn't bear it any longer and even poked their own ears.
Some of the foreign races realized that something was wrong. They began to flip through the ancient records, and as they flipped through, they found a terrifying answer.
This was probably the "Final Thunder" before the arrival of the Apocalypse.
This meant that the end was coming!
Panic quickly spread to every corner of the mother world.
Some of the alien races held ceremonies and prayed to the heavens. Some of them began to move and hide underground. But even more of them fell into endless fear and could do nothing. Everything in the Chaos was about to be destroyed. Where could they run to?
However, some of the stronger Nirvana Realm experts had a glimmer of hope in their hearts.
They had traveled further in the Nirvana Realm, and they had learned some things from the Saint Soul Realm and Immortal Realm experts. They also knew how powerful the current ruler of their mother world, the brutal Evil Deities, was.
Perhaps he could fight against the Apocalypse.
When people had no choice, they would hold on tightly to their only hope. Soon, all the knowledgeable people in the mother world turned their attention to the Evil Deities.
It was the same in the mother world. Luo Zheng's Land of Origin couldn't escape it either.
After only a few dozen breaths, the Land of Origin was also shrouded in continuous sound waves.
However, the internal structure of the Land of Origin was much simpler than that of the mother world.
Other than the continental plate, Mount Li, and the Ultimate cemetery, the other parts were very empty. Luo Zheng used an extreme form of energy to construct a huge enchantment to shroud them.
Although the enchantment couldn't completely block the sound waves, after two defenses, the sound was very soft.
The disturbance caused by the sound waves was reduced, but panic inevitably began to spread.
The Ultimate cemetery, the divine region, and the continental plate were still fine. Most of them didn't even know what the Apocalypse was, and they continued to live their lives.
However, the people in Mount Li came from the mother world. Some of the elites among them had some understanding. After knowing that the Apocalypse was coming, of course they would be afraid.
Nuwa didn't hide the news of the Apocalypse. Instead, she made it public.
Regardless of whether they were panicking or not, everyone had to face this moment. She felt that everyone should know.
At the top of the divine region, many Saints gathered together. They had been summoned by Luo Xiao, and they didn't know what had happened.
Luo Xiao stood with his hands behind his back. With a solemn expression, he told them about the Apocalypse, which sounded new, and then told them that the Apocalypse had arrived.
The expressions of the Saints, who were originally calm, changed drastically.
The divine region was so peaceful now. They never thought that such a disaster would happen!
These Saints were like the knowledgeable people in the mother world. They quickly placed their hopes on Luo Zheng.
"Your son can create a world that's so many times bigger than the divine region! Can't he deal with the Apocalypse? "
"Yes, is there anything in the Chaos that Luo Zheng can't do?"
"We believe in Luo Zheng …"
These Saints were very determined. After all, he was their only hope.

Chapter 3866
A long time ago, Ji Xuanyuan, Shennong, and "Sky" had also destroyed the Mother World.
They used the flames of "Extremely Small Space" to destroy three hundred provinces in a row.
But it was not destruction in the strict sense … Although life was burned, the material was still there, and the foundation of material was still there.
Even the sea that was evaporated by the flames eventually turned into heavy rain and returned to the ground.
But the Evil Deities turned all the material in a world into pure energy and used it to create a huge shuttle hole. This kind of destruction was irreversible! Facing the aggressive Downsiders, the Evil Deities seemed to have only one way.
In the Land of Extinction, a blue light film unfolded in front of the Downsiders.
In the center of the light film was a huge sphere. This sphere was the Mother World, but the surface of the Mother World was covered with dark blue patches.
"Hahaha …" The Downsiders let out a series of low laughter. "Consuming a huge amount of material to turn into energy is no doubt drinking poison to quench thirst. How many rounds can you last?"
On the Jade Platform, Luo Zheng also had a blue light film in front of him. He also noticed the predicament of the Evil Deities.
"Looking at this situation, the Evil Deities have consumed more than a hundred provinces, but the people in the Mother World are pitiful." Luo Zheng showed pity.
"From the perspective of the Evil Deities, he is not wrong at all." Ku Shu blinked and said, "If these provinces are not sacrificed, all the people in the Mother World will be wiped out!"
"But once the Mother World's material is exhausted, he will definitely lose." Nuwa judged.
Luo Zheng nodded and agreed with Nuwa's judgment.
Wen Dizhi, who had been quiet all this time, said. "Not necessarily …" Ku Shu, Luo Zheng, Nuwa, and the others all looked at him.
As the master of the House of Great Guessing, Wen Dizhi was so knowledgeable that even Ku Shu was inferior to him. He was afraid that he had other ideas.
"Can you all easily build space channels?"
Wen Dizhi asked.
Fuxi and the others nodded.
Wen Dizhi stretched out two fingers, and the power of space burst out from his fingertips. A thin space channel formed on his fingertips.
Then, a second tunnel, a third tunnel, a fourth tunnel … In a short period of time, thousands of inch-long spatial tunnels had been constructed.
"Wen Dizhi, what are you doing?"
Ku Shu's face was filled with confusion.
Wen Di did not answer and continued to construct spatial passageways. When he had constructed more than one thousand and five hundred spatial passageways, the space between his hands suddenly became extremely unstable. The spatial passageways that had been orderly tangled together and then exploded.
But the moment it exploded, Wen Dizhi rubbed his hands gently, and the exploding energy ball disappeared in his hands.
"What do you mean?
Is it a magic trick? "
Fuxi was a little confused.
Nuwa was the first to react. "The number of spatial passageways in the same area is limited. If there are too many of them, they will collapse …" Nuwa had done a similar experiment before, but she had not thought of this point.
"That is to say …" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
"The spatial power contained in the Round-trip Caverns is even stronger. If there are too many Round-trip Caverns in the same area, it will collapse more easily," Wen Dizhi deduced.
"How many trips can this area take?"
Ku Shu asked.
Wen Dizhi shook his head. "I can't predict. Maybe twenty, thirty, or even more …" "If the Round-trip Caverns collapse, will the Needle of Destiny in the Round-trip Caverns be destroyed?"
Fuxi asked.
Wen Dizhi still shook his head.
He could only speculate based on his own knowledge. It was impossible for him to be so precise.
Luo Zheng released twenty-one Round-trip Caverns … The Degrader released twenty-two more … The Evil Deities released twenty-three … The competition after the Arrival of Destiny became boring.
The only variable was the Evil Deities that were struggling to hold on.
Every time he transformed a portion of a great province, the Saint Soul and Immortal experts he had invited over would reveal sorrowful expressions. However, none of them dared to say a word. Their lives didn't belong to them in the Origin World. If they were unhappy, they would be transformed into energy! Tongtian did not say anything, but his face was also very serious. Judging from this trend, they had already lost.
Not long after, the Degrader released thirty-one Round-trip Caverns. Sixty-two huge caverns appeared in this area. The Needle seemed to be moving in a maze. It was impossible to tell which part of it was in front and which part was behind.
The Evil Deities transformed more than thirty large areas without hesitation and released a black hole.
At this moment, the black hole's surroundings were slightly distorted.
Strangely, the distorted ripples of this black hole appeared in other black holes at the same time! Seeing this, the Evil Deities smiled. "Finally …" The Evil Deities had tens of thousands of clones, and they were all very smart.
Under the arrangement of the Evil Deities, each of the Evil Deities thought independently and carried out all kinds of strange experiments. Spatial experiments were a very popular category.
Therefore, the Evil Deities knew very well that the Round-trip Caverns would collapse very quickly. They had been waiting for this moment! "Hua!"
The Evil Deities released another white hole, and ripples appeared around the white hole.
When the ripples spread to other white holes, all the Round-trip Caverns began to twist left and right, sometimes expanding and sometimes shrinking. These short but huge space tunnels became extremely unstable! The Needle, as a rigid body, would definitely conflict with all the Round-trip Caverns.
Some Round-trip Caverns tightly wrapped around the Needle when they shrank. The friction between the space and the Needle made a harsh sound wave in the Chaotic World. When the Round-trip Caverns expanded, they also collided with the Needle, causing even more intense space distortion! The space became extremely chaotic … "It has really begun! There are only thirty-two Round-trip Caverns, and the space can't bear it anymore! "
Taishang Laojun was surprised.
Luo Nian stared at the black and white holes and asked, "The Round-trip Caverns seem to be unable to do anything to the Needle. This Needle is so hard!"
Wen Dizhi's face turned pale. "If the Needle is cut off by the Round-trip Caverns, it will be fine. The Three Places of Origin will only be hit by the space storm … What if it can't be cut off?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"The Needle will bear a huge stress and be pressed into a real bend by the space force." Wen Dizhi stretched out a bent finger. "Then … straighten!"
He flicked his finger hard.
This action was very vivid.
The Needle would become a steel cable that suddenly popped out, and the huge stress released would destroy everything it touched! When Wen Dizhi just demonstrated, the thirty-two Round-trip Caverns collapsed at the same time, leaving only pieces of the Needle floating in the air. These Needles were not broken, but were just an illusion formed by the chaos of space.
At the moment when the Round-trip Caverns collapsed, the Needles twisted like frantically struggling leeches.

Chapter 3867
The twisted "needle" itself was a whole. If they wanted to forcibly break through the collapsed space, they would have to reconnect as a whole again.
This was a battle between volume and rules. The "needle" represented an indestructible volume, but the space had been directly divided. The aftermath of this level of battle was extremely powerful.
Fan-shaped, awn-shaped, and even fist-shaped shock waves burst out in all directions.
"Hua, rumble, rumble, rumble …" A huge claw-shaped shock wave rolled toward Luo Zheng's Land of Origin, fiercely cutting off a large piece of the surface of the Land of Origin. A thorn-like shock wave even left a large crater dozens of miles deep on the surface of the Land of Origin.
Luo Zheng's Land of Origin and the Degraded One's Land of Extinction had retained their original state, so they could withstand the shock.
However, the Evil Gods had converted the matter of many large continents into energy, and many parts of the homeworld were only covered with a thin layer of skin. When these shockwaves hit, they easily tore apart the not-so-thick layers of the homeworld.
In a few breaths' time, the surface of the mother world was riddled with holes, like a sieve.
However, the matter in these areas had been hollowed out, and the creatures were destroyed. A large hole was not important. However, the face that the Evil Deities carefully protected was exposed in front of everyone.
"Guang, guang, guang …" After the needle twisted for a while, it finally broke through the rules of space.
The surrounding space was broken apart by the "needle" itself, and the pieces of the "needle" began to reconnect.
After the broken "needle" was reconnected, the body of the "needle" was still in a crooked state. This was because the "needle" was forcibly distorted by the broken shuttle hole.
Now, relying on its own strong toughness, it began to restore its straight state, so it was like an uncontrollable long whip whipping around! It attacked indiscriminately! "Woo …" In the sound of metal stretching, a small part of the middle of the "needle" actually whipped toward the Land of Extinction.
"This …" At this moment, the Degraded One wanted to curse.
They clearly had the absolute advantage … He felt that the mother world was not qualified to participate in this battle at all and should be the first to be eliminated.
They were clearly struggling at death's door … Why did it suddenly become like this?
"Woo …" Seeing the curved "needle" coming at him at an extremely fast speed, the Downtrodden habitually built a "return hole". However, the space in this area was still in chaos and could not form a new return hole.
"Stop!"
The Subdued One then controlled the formation plate in front of him. The entire formation plate was covered in a red light.
He wanted the "needle" to stop.
The Downgraded could control the extending speed of the "needle," reduce the extending speed to zero, and even force the "needle" to retract. However, he could not control the direction of the "needle" like he could control his fingers.
Moreover, releasing the enormous stress contained in the needle itself was even more out of his control.
Control failed.
One side of the needle flew towards the Land of Extinction.
Crack! Boom boom boom … "The large units collided with each other, creating a thunderous sound.
One of the three corners of the Land of Extinction was "lifted", and that corner was directly smashed into pieces.
Inside this corner was a huge space. Inside this space were countless chaotic creatures. They were originally still clamoring and full of fighting spirit, preparing to welcome the upcoming battle.
But before the real battle even began, they saw the sky collapse … The shattered "sky" mixed with a gigantic silver object falling down. These chaotic creatures with extremely tenacious life forces were wiped out in an instant, and they lost their lives before they could even let out a scream.
Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh … The Land of Extinction was originally in a stagnant state, but after a corner was smashed by the "needle," the remaining kinetic energy pushed it to rotate.
Fortunately, the curved part of the "needle" straightened instantly and did not cause further damage to the Land of Extinction.
However, there were still many curved parts of the "needle" that danced without any pattern.
On the Emerald Platform, Luo Zheng and the others were stunned at this scene.
Although Wen Dizhi had already predicted this move, it was two different concepts to predict and realize it.
"Evil Deities, this guy …" "He planned this from the beginning?
That's terrifying! "
"He will accidentally hurt himself like this. This is a gamble with his life …" Nuwa said with a complicated expression.
Luo Zheng did not have time to speak. He focused on controlling the Land of Origin to dodge.
Only one corner of the Land of Extinction was lifted, and one-fifth of it was destroyed. If the "needle" hit him directly, he would be doomed eternally.
However, the Land of Origin was so huge that it was not easy to move quickly.
In the process of moving slowly, the "needle" suddenly bounced, and the curved head pierced directly towards the Land of Origin. The speed was more than a hundred times faster than before! Everyone on the Emerald Platform looked up at the same time.
A huge sharp angle appeared in the bloody sky, as if a pair of scissors had cut open the bloody cloth, revealing the dark outer space of the Chaotic World.
That huge "needle" cut a huge hole in the Land of Origin before swaggering off! Everyone breathed a sigh of relief.
If the needle had struck from top to bottom, the Land of Origin would have been split into two halves. Now, it was only a small hole, which was good luck … The fatal "needle" cut through the Land of Origin and surged backward, heading towards the mother world at an even faster speed. However, when the "needle" was still dozens of miles away from the mother world, it brushed past the mother world and turned around to hit the Land of Extinction! In the black hall, Evil Deities covered his face and laughed wildly.
The shrill laughter lingered in everyone's ears.
When facing an opponent that was difficult to defeat, he dragged the situation into a chaotic situation where no one could see clearly.
This was what he was best at, and it was also a huge gamble! When the Deities saw the "needle" coming straight towards them, they felt as if they had eaten shit.
Since it was a gamble of luck, why was luck not on their side?
Now that the Land of Extinction was in the path of the "needle," the Land of Extinction would probably be smashed into pieces if the "needle" struck it! "Come back!"
The Deities were quick-witted and controlled the disk to force the "needle" to retract.
The length of the "needle" was shrinking at a speed visible to the naked eye, and by the time the "needle" hit the Land of Extinction, it had shortened by quite a bit.
Therefore, the "needle" did not hit the Land of Extinction, and only relied on the tip of the needle to create a crack on the surface of the Land of Extinction before bouncing back in the opposite direction.
"It's your turn to die …" The Deities sneered.
The mother world was right in the direction of the "needle" bouncing back.
The Deities added fuel to the fire, and controlled the disk to make the "needle" extend rapidly.
"Whoosh …" The "needle" turned into an unimaginably huge whip and lashed towards the mother world. The mother world was easily smashed like a fragile egg.

Chapter 3868
After the collapse of the mother world, a massive amount of fragments were released.
The large fragments were several million miles long and wide, while the small fragments were thousands of miles long, forming a diffuse band of fragments that floated up and down.
After the "needle" smashed the mother world into pieces, it bounced in the opposite direction due to inertia, but the rebound force was smaller and smaller, and finally returned to its original position, becoming a straight "needle".
Fuxi, Nuwa, Elder Ying, Donghuang Taiyi, and the other natives of the mother world looked at the shattered mother world with sadness in their hearts.
After all, it was the world that gave birth to them, and in the end, it could not avoid the tragedy of being destroyed by The End.
However, they did not have much time to wallow in sadness.
After the "needle" returned to its straight state, it began to extend again, and its target was the Land of Origin! And the Land of Extinction also accelerated to catch up, and kept parallel to the Land of Origin.
After half an hour, the "needle" was very close to the Land of Origin.
Just as it was about to plunge into the Land of Origin, a new shuttle hole was formed again.
The Land of Origin had left the area of spatial disorder, and Luo Zheng easily released a shuttle hole.
The black hole sucked the "needle" in, and the white hole spat it out.
And the direction the white hole spat out was the Land of Extinction that was flying parallel to the Land of Origin! Just as the needle approached the Land of Extinction, the same scene appeared again, and the shuttle hole sent the "needle" to the Land of Origin.
The situation seemed to have returned to its previous state, but the mother world had been eliminated, and the fierce battle between the three became a game of two.
The key was that both sides were moving, so they could avoid the collapse of space due to the excessive number of shuttle holes.
And in the case of unlimited energy, as long as both sides were willing, they could summon an infinite number of shuttle holes, and let the "needle" fold an infinite number of times! "Whoosh …" "Whoosh …" The huge "needle" moved back and forth in a zigzag pattern, and the sound of tides surging in space was heard.
Because a corner of the Land of Extinction was broken, the sound was clearly transmitted inside.

"Me?
How? "
The Spirit Snake King was confused.
If the two worlds collided, the Spirit Snake King would definitely be the one to lead the charge.
But now it was a contest between two worlds, and it could only be a spectator.
"This."
The Subdued One pointed his finger, and a white ring appeared around the Spirit Snake King's neck. This was the prototype of a white hole.
The Snake Spirit King looked down at the white ring and asked in confusion, "And then?"

"You want me … to send you off?"
The Snake Spirit King's eyes were filled with astonishment.
"Of course," the Demoted One said coldly. Obviously, there was no room for negotiation. The Spirit Snake King could only bite the bullet and agree.
Afterward, the Spirit Snake King's body became fainter and fainter until it completely blended into the environment like clear water. The Degrader helped the Spirit Snake King completely hide before sending it into the Chaotic World.
When the needle was close to the Land of Extinction, the Spirit Snake King quietly climbed onto the needle.

Luo Zheng did not notice the existence of the Spirit Snake King. He still released a round trip hole step by step, ready to continue sending the needle over.
But at this moment, a tiny snake shadow peeled off from the needle.
The moment the Spirit Snake King left the needle, it released the white ring on its head. In the blink of an eye, the white ring expanded into a huge white hole. The Land of Extinction drilled out of the white hole and crashed into the Land of Origin.
The Spirit Snake King was in the center of the white hole and was swept into the Land of Origin.
"Rumble …" The Land of Origin shook, Li Mountain shook, and the Emerald Terrace shook violently.
Whether it was Luo Zheng, Ku Shu, Nuwa, or the others, they did not expect such a change.
They stared blankly as the bloody sky was torn apart and the sharp horn of the Land of Extinction pierced into it. At the same time, they saw a huge snake recover its body after rolling over. It was the Evil Spirit Snake King.
Although the Spirit Snake King was dizzy, it was very happy when it found that it had been knocked into the Land of Origin. The Mother World had been destroyed, and this was its last shelter! "We are in, Master Degrader!"
The Spirit Snake King said.
The Degrader did not respond. A blue space door appeared on the sharp horn of the Land of Extinction, and groups of black chaotic creatures rushed out. There were so many of them that when they rushed out, they looked like a swarm of locusts. From afar, they looked like clouds of black fog.
"Buzz …" A figure appeared in front of the Spirit Snake King out of thin air. It was one of Luo Zheng's Inner Incarnations.
"We meet again," the Spirit Snake King opened its mouth. "Today, I will avenge my humiliation …" As soon as its voice fell, a powerful force locked its huge snake body. At the same time, Luo Zheng said, "This is my territory. Here, you are not my opponent …" Then Luo Zheng stretched out his finger and gently pinched it. The light of destruction enveloped the Spirit Snake King, and at the same time, the strength of the power that locked the Spirit Snake King increased crazily.
Although the Spirit Snake King's Treacherous Body was extremely strong, it was still crushed by the power and dissipated inch by inch … Previously, the Spirit Snake King had the advantage in the Jade Clear Sky by relying on its "omniscience" ability. Now, Luo Zheng had the power of omniscience and omnipotence.
"Awooo …" "Gulajili!"
"..." Chaotic beings continuously poured out of the spatial gate. Their numbers had already reached hundreds of thousands.
The first group of chaotic creatures had already discovered Luo Zheng. They roared as they rushed towards Luo Zheng.
"You are even less qualified …" Luo Zheng tilted his head slightly and slashed at them.
The power transformed into invisible blades that shot towards these chaotic creatures.
"Swish swish swish swish swish …" The invisible blades were extremely sharp. These chaotic creatures did not know what was happening. They only heard the sound of wind whistling in their ears, and then they were dismembered into large and small pieces … Black blood and broken limbs tumbled in the air, splashing everywhere … Hundreds of thousands of chaotic creatures were dealt with in an instant.
However, the space doors were still open, and the chaotic creatures were still coming out.
While Luo Zheng was slaughtering these chaotic creatures, he had some doubts in his heart.
The Degrader knew that sending these cannon fodder was meaningless, but he still did it. What was his goal?

Chapter 3869
Luo Zheng raised his right hand slightly.
Even more invisible blades were quickly produced and shot towards the spatial gates.
After penetrating some of the chaotic creatures, they hit the surface of the Space Gates.

The Space Gates were not solid and were as fragile as glass, shattering with a single hit.

The chaotic creatures were still swarming out!
Luo Zheng snorted coldly, still sparing no effort to attack.
He was only expending energy, but the Land of Annihilation was expending lives.
Although the number of these chaotic creatures was terrifying, they could not withstand the slaughter like cutting rice …
As the number of Space Gates increased, Luo Zheng also brought out more and more invisible blades. In a breath's time, he could kill tens of thousands of chaotic creatures and destroy dozens of Space Gates.
After the slaughter lasted for dozens of breaths, Luo Zheng suddenly realized something was wrong.
The black remains of the chaotic creatures did not fall to the ground after being shattered. The remains floated around as if weightless. From a distance, they looked like black insects of various shapes, covering a considerable area.

A Luo Zheng appeared outside the contaminated area again. I've never used this kind of energy before, so I'm just testing it. Why are you so happy?"
Luo Zheng's real body was on the Emerald Terrace. What appeared now was just an incarnation of his body, which he could have as many incarnations as he wanted.

He raised his hand slightly, and balls of light the size of marbles condensed behind him.

The balls of light rained down on the black wreckage.
When the balls of light touched the black wreckage, they sank in.
The black color of the wreckage quickly faded and returned to the original color of flesh and blood.
Countless white and red wreckage floated in the air, adding a terrifying presence. But the contaminated space returned to its original owner.
If Luo Zheng didn't have the Skyring Staff, he wouldn't be able to do anything against the Extreme Domain Climbing State. He could only watch as the Degraders contaminated and consumed the Land of Origin. Now, Luo Zheng could easily counter them.
"We have the advantage of home ground. Even if the Land of Extinction barges in, we can't do anything!"
Nuwa smiled. The situation was better than she had imagined.

The Snake Spirit King's voice sounded again.
At the same time …
One after another, even larger spatial doors appeared.
After the large spatial doors opened, giant chaotic creatures came out. Their shapes were still strange. There were multi-headed snakes, long-armed ghosts, multi-legged tarantulas, and so on.

These chaotic creatures were no different from the chaotic creatures before except for their large size.
He didn't even change his attack method. He still used a large number of invisible blades to drown these giants.

Large and small Chaos creatures marched forward wave after wave, fearlessly sacrificing their lives one after another.
Millions of them …

Wave after wave of black wreckage was purified and turned into countless flesh and blood wreckage. The Degrader wanted to pollute the Land of Origin with the Extreme Domain Climbing State. It was a delusion after all.
Wen Dizhi had been silently observing this massacre. Although the Extreme Domain Climbing State was resolved by Luo Zheng, he still faintly felt that something was wrong.
The Degrader was extremely intelligent. It was impossible for him to do something useless.
Using these chaotic creatures to attract everyone's attention?

The real Land of Extinction was enough to swallow up two Lands of Origin …
Swallow …

Dizhi? "

Chapter 3870
When a corner of the Land of Extinction crashed into the Land of Beginnings and caused a huge commotion, it attracted everyone's attention.
Luo Zheng was even more focused on killing the chaotic creatures and purifying the energy that climbed to the Extreme State.
Now that he heard Wen Dizhi's words, Luo Zheng realized that something was wrong and wanted to find out.

The consciousness created by the Illusion Space Vision quickly spread out in all directions.
This consciousness spread so fast that it could reach the end of the Chaotic World in a short time.
But this time, it encountered an obstacle. As soon as the consciousness left the Land of Beginnings, it fell into a muddy sea as if it was completely absorbed by a mysterious force.
"How is this possible?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment.
That wisp of consciousness had spread out in countless directions. Even if the consciousness in one direction was captured and eliminated, what about the consciousness in the other directions?
The Land of Beginnings was so huge now that unless the Land of Beginnings was wrapped in something bigger, the consciousness could not be completely absorbed.
Wrapped … Luo Zheng's heart pounded.
He looked up at the bloody sky and suddenly realized something. His face became extremely ugly.
"Father, what's wrong?"
Luo Nian noticed Luo Zheng's ugly expression.
Not only did Luo Nian notice it, but so did the others.
Luo Zheng did not answer Luo Nian's question. He narrowed his eyes slightly and created a doppelganger at the edge of the Land of Beginnings.
"Whoosh …" Luo Zheng's doppelganger did not stop after it appeared. It stretched out its palm and placed it on the inner wall of the Land of Beginnings.
Now the inner wall of the Land of Beginnings was very thick. This was the result of absorbing countless heads in the Chaotic World. Even the Immortal Realm could not break the inner wall and enter the Chaotic World. But for Luo Zheng, this was not an obstacle.

Luo Zheng's doppelganger softly spat out a word.
His body went straight into it and quickly passed through the walls of the Land of Beginnings to the Outer Realm.
Logically speaking, this should be an empty Chaotic World … but now he saw a black mountain range in the distance. These mountains were completely upside down, and the peak of the mountain was facing Luo Zheng's doppelganger.
"Wow!"

Chapter 3874
