Evil Deities thought for a while and said, "About four million."
Each clone was far stronger than an ordinary Immortal Realm cultivator, so the Mother World could only create millions of clones.
"With so many clones, it's a force that can't be ignored," Tongtian said.
Evil Deities sneered, "Four million ants are no match for an elephant. In terms of combat power, they are just a motley crew. I only need their intelligence."
One of the doppelgangers of Evil Deities on the other side of the prison fence turned around and said, "If you only need our wisdom, then this body is meaningless. You only need our heads!"
This doppelganger's words reminded Evil Deities of something. Evil Deities' expression appeared on his face as he said, "That's a good suggestion. Then let's just keep their heads!"
If he only had a head, he wouldn't even need a prison.
The Prime of Tongtian thought that the Evil Deities were just a joke, but he did not expect that the Prime of Tongtian would start the sacrifice again.
Some of the Evil Deities were in agreement. They were more inclined to pursue the most fundamental shortcut of energy. Some of the Evil Deities were very resistant and even cursed wildly. But no matter how these clones reacted, their bodies quickly disintegrated into pure energy.
Only rows of heads were left on the huge platform. At the same time, more heads of Evil Deities were quickly created.
The Prime of Tongtian and Han Ge were both dumbfounded.
They were not beings who followed rules, but seeing Evil Deities' arrangement, they were so shocked that they could not close their mouths.
What was he trying to do?
Evil Deities provided these heads with energy to survive, ensuring that they could think flexibly.
"The number has suddenly increased by 400 times. There are about 1.6 billion heads." Evil Deities finally smiled.
He actually wanted to create more heads, but he needed to reserve some energy for these heads to think. 1.6 billion was basically the limit.
The doppelganger who had just made the suggestion used his remaining head to suggest again, "Actually, the number can still be increased. We only need one brain. It's more efficient to communicate with Divine Sense."
When the Prime of Tongtian and Han Ge heard this suggestion, they were shocked.
No way?
Not only were there rows of heads, but there were also rows of brains?
"That's right! Eyes, ears, mouth, nose, skull, teeth, these can also be sacrificed! "
Evil Deities clapped his hands.
Thus, 1.6 billion heads were sacrificed again.
After the sacrifice, the 1.6 billion doppelganger were only left with the brains that contained the souls. The number also increased to 2 billion.
These brains all had souls stronger than the Holy Soul Realm. They all released their Divine Sense to scan the surroundings. With so many Divine Senses intertwined, a chaotic soul storm was formed in the sky.
"All of you, quiet down!"
After the Cthulhu's main body released a terrifying soul pressure, the two billion brains retracted their consciousness.
Next, Evil Deities divided these brains into several large areas. One part of the brain was responsible for thinking, another part was responsible for collecting and summarizing, and another part was responsible for filtering useful information and so on.
The 2 billion brain units contained a lot of messy information. They had to be organized.
Just as he finished arranging this, he suddenly turned around and looked into the distance. There was a silver flash there.
The Evil God raised his eyebrows fiercely. He reached out and pulled the black ship and the platform in the air, dragging both of them dozens of miles away. Then, a terrifying silver energy stream flew past! The energy stream passed by at a terrifying speed and quickly disappeared into the void. Tongtian and Han Ge were shocked when they saw the energy stream.
"This, this is the energy stream you were talking about … Why does it have such terrifying power?!"
Evil Deities glanced at them coolly. "This is also why I'm burning my boats."
The battle between Luo Zheng and the Downtrodden continued in the burial ground.
In a short time, Luo Zheng released six Mirror Annihilation in a row, blasting six large holes in the burial ground.
Although the Mirror Annihilation was terrifying, it couldn't completely eliminate the Downtrodden.
The Subdued One followed closely behind, releasing a blinding golden sun. The golden halo surrounded the Subdued One from the inside out, expanding to the periphery of the Tomb of Extinction. Blackfoal Mountain, the Celestial domain, and the Sinkhole graveyard were all devoured by the golden sun. Billions of chaotic beings perished.
Apart from the immortal residence Luo Zheng was protecting, the entire burial ground was deathly silent.
When the golden sun's light gradually dimmed, the Downtrodden's figure slowly emerged from it. "The end of every world is a battle with me. It's also the time I enjoy the most. But this time, it's you. How boring …" In the Downtrodden's eyes, Luo Zheng only knew how to use Mirror Annihilation randomly. It couldn't do any real damage to him.
"Ten billion …" Luo Zheng raised his hand slightly, and ten billion silver rings appeared in the sky.
Realm, Annihilation, Merge … Whoosh … The gathered energy stream once again charged at the Downtrodden.
This time, the Downtrodden didn't dodge. He planned to forcibly block the energy stream.
But just as the Downtrodden was resisting the energy stream, Luo Zheng's figure silently disappeared. The next moment, he appeared beside the black light.

Chapter 3892
According to the judgment of Evil Deities, he wanted to find the answer by restoring the energy to the world of the thirteen cells.
However, when the energy was restored to the twelfth cell, Evil Deities had already grasped a hint of the mystery.
There were two types of energy in the world of the twelve cells. One was creation, and the other was destruction. They had no special characteristics. It could even be said that the two types of energy were the same.
When the energy was restored to the thirteenth cell, the situation became even simpler.
There was no energy in the thirteenth cell … The only mystery of the thirteenth cell was that it controlled "all" authority.
When the Evil God came into contact with this authority, he discovered the boundless energy hidden in the void.
"It's so beautiful …" Shen Tu looked at the colorful ribbons floating in the sky and sighed.
Hearing her words, Evil Deities reached out and gently pointed at Shen Tu's index finger. A small band of dark red light appeared. It looked like a delicate ring.
"This is …" Shen Tu looked at the band of light in surprise.
"It's equivalent to the energy of the mother world sealed in it. You can use it at any time," Evil Deities smiled gently. "However, it shouldn't be necessary …" Although the mother world contained a lot of energy, Evil Deities would have to face either Luo Zheng or the Degraded One. The level of energy involved in the battle against such an opponent was "infinite". The energy on Shen Tu's finger was not enough.
Even so, Shen Tu was so excited that her eyes turned red.
Turning the entire mother world into a ring was the most romantic thing for her.
"If the energy of 'field' is in the shape of a band of light, what is the shape of 'scale'?"
Tongtian asked.
He was also a person with a strong desire for knowledge, so he wanted to take this opportunity to see it.
"Scale … It's an unequal sharp angle of energy."
"This is it!"
Irregular triangular energy blocks appeared in the sky.
"Decoupling is a kind of block-like energy … This kind of energy can't be seen from the outside. It's a complete whole when it appears, so it's very difficult to control. I don't know how to fight in the eighth cell."
Evil Deities said as it released pieces of "decoupling" energy. These pieces of "decoupling" energy had a complex shape. They looked like intricate boxes because there was almost no energy escaping from them.
"The ninth grid world's' Mo 'is more interesting, it doesn't have a fixed form … The tenth grid …" Although the two billion brains of the Evil Deities restored the energy bit by bit, without the support of unlimited energy, the energy could only exist in the brain.
Evil Deities were also very curious about these energies, and now they were revealing them layer by layer.
Meanwhile, the battle in the Extinct Burial Grounds was still in a stalemate.
"Boom …" A layer of bright red glass barrier was shattered.
Then, the Downtrodden fell into the illusion created by the extreme state of mind, and he could only use extreme state energy to resist this illusion.
Just as he broke out of the illusion, he found himself sealed in an extremely sturdy copper ball.
"Are you done yet?"
"Whoosh …" He had already broken more than a hundred layers of various barriers and seals.
To Luo Zheng, these barriers and seals were extremely easy and convenient. With the support of unlimited energy, let alone three hundred, three thousand, or even thirty thousand layers could go on forever.
At the same time, he was also monitoring the Downtrodden in the black light. When the Downtrodden attacked the innermost layer of the barrier, Luo Zheng would provide that layer with unlimited energy.
This way, the Downtrodden would have to expend a lot of effort to break each layer of the barrier, and he wouldn't be able to destroy all the outer layers in one go.
After the Downtrodden attacked randomly, he found that the number of layers of barriers and seals exceeded his expectations. He stopped temporarily to think of a way to break out.
What trapped him now was an "ice tomb". After being provided with unlimited extreme state energy, the ice tomb could freeze anything that came close. It was very scary.
But in the Downtrodden's eyes, it was a chance for him to escape.
This ice tomb was strong, but its weakness was obvious. It was completely countered by Xin Huo's extreme state. Even if the ice tomb was buffed with unlimited energy, it could still be easily broken.
"Huu …" Xin Huo's extreme state began to brew in his hands.
His slender arm suddenly reached out of the black light and swept towards the ice tomb.
"Break!"
"Huala!"
The huge ice tomb directly collapsed.
The moment Luo Zheng felt the ice tomb collapsing, he wanted to concentrate the unlimited energy on the next layer of the seal. But the Downtrodden came prepared and was faster.
"Shatter all the barriers in the back!"
"Boom …" Balls of flames formed an irresistible torrent and erupted.
Without the support of unlimited energy, the outer layers of the barrier couldn't resist at all. All the seals and barriers were like paper, collapsing under the impact of the torrent of flames. "Huala …" After destroying all the barriers, the Downtrodden's voice once again echoed in the entire Land of Extinction.
"You want to suppress me in the Source Light with such a small trick? You're dreaming! Since the Land of Extinction can protect you, I will cripple it. How are you going to fight me then? "
The black light could allow the Downtrodden to be reborn infinitely, but the Land of Extinction could also allow Luo Zheng to be reborn infinitely.
The Land of Origin even gave Luo Zheng more power. This was originally an unequal battle.
But now, the Downtrodden realized that he couldn't kill Luo Zheng without destroying the Land of Extinction.
"Weng …" In the blink of an eye, the Downtrodden had arrived at the edge of the Land of Extinction.
A golden sun rose from the Downtrodden's body, devouring everything around it and burning a huge hole in the Land of Extinction.
The Downtrodden flew along the edge of the Land of Extinction. Under the golden sun's devouring, a huge hole was created on the surface of the Land of Extinction.
Luo Zheng's body flashed, trying to stop the Downtrodden.
The Downtrodden didn't stop, his body flickered and appeared on the other side of the Land of Extinction to continue devouring.
Looking from afar in the Chaotic World, it looked like a huge worm was eating holes in the Land of Extinction.
When the holes accumulated to a certain extent, the Land of Extinction finally collapsed.
Luo Zheng couldn't stop it. He could only watch it happen.
However, the moment the Ultimate Burial Land collapsed, Luo Zheng had a strange feeling in his heart. He felt that he had merged into an even larger Land of Beginnings.
There should be a Chaotic World outside the Land of Extinction.
How could there be another Land of Origin?
Luo Zheng was extremely confused.
"Are you surprised?"
Evil Deities' voice sounded in his ears.
As Luo Zheng looked around, Evil Deities' figure slowly condensed not far away. His eyes were filled with mockery. "You can't get rid of the Downtrodden after so long. What a piece of trash. Let me do it for you."

Chapter 3893
Luo Zheng stared at Evil Deities' face as if he didn't recognize him. "Where … is this?"
He wondered if he had fallen into an illusion created by the Downtrodden.
"Here?
It's inside the Mother World, "Evil Deities shrugged.
Luo Zheng looked around. Other than the fragments of the Land of Extinction, there was only a dark void.
"This is the Chaotic World," Luo Zheng frowned.
"Your Land of Origin has been integrated into the Mother World. You and I have the same authority. Why don't you sense the range of the Mother World?"
Evil Deities said with a smile.
Luo Zheng closed his eyes doubtfully. With a slight movement of his mind, he sensed a huge "Land of Origin".
His figure flashed slightly, and he was already at the edge of the Land of Origin. What he saw was a triangular stone and a doppelganger of Evil Deities sitting on the stone.
The doppelganger of Evil Deities was not taken back, nor was it sacrificed. He sat alone on the triangular stone, teasing a rock snake. This rock snake was previously hidden in the triangular stone and was brought all the way here.
Compared to the two billion Evil Deities who only had one brain, this doppelganger was undoubtedly considered lucky.
"Yo, esteemed guest," Evil Deities' doppelganger greeted Luo Zheng after glancing at him.
Luo Zheng's gaze went past Evil Deities' doppelganger and looked at the world behind him. That was the inner wall of the Chaotic World!
"This is the edge of the Chaotic World?"
Luo Zheng asked.
Evil Deities' doppelganger reached out and slapped the inner wall of the Chaotic World. "Can't you see?"
"How did you do it?"
Luo Zheng asked.
Evil Deities' doppelganger shrugged. "Ask my Lord, I don't know either …"
Luo Zheng returned to Evil Deities' doppelganger with a complicated expression. In fact, Luo Zheng already knew the general situation through his omniscient ability. The Mother World was gone, and only the black ship, the two billion brains on the square platform, and the six stones scattered at the edge of the Chaotic World were left.
"You turned the Chaotic World into a Land of Origin with those six stones?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"You can say that," Evil Deities' doppelganger replied faintly.
"This … how is it possible …"
Although Luo Zheng had personally verified it, he still found it hard to believe.
Looking at Luo Zheng's doppelganger, the doppelganger's doppelganger.
"In the past, when I was a living being in your inner world, I had great limitations and couldn't understand anything in the outside world. Now, you have the same understanding of the power I have. What you know is just a drop in the ocean. I can make you …"
Before he finished speaking, a black fog suddenly shrouded Evil Deities and Luo Zheng.
"Probability Extreme State!"
"I want you to die!"
After destroying the Land of Extinction, the Deities waited for the opportunity to give Luo Zheng a fatal blow.

"Bang!"

"Again!"
Evil Deities gritted its teeth and roared, using all the energy in its body to perform the Space Diversion.
"Boom!"
Its limited power couldn't shake the square space.
"Don't waste your energy. You know very well that you can't escape." Luo Zheng slowly floated down.
Using the flow of energy to deal with Evil Deities. This was a move that even Luo Zheng had never thought of, but it was surprisingly effective.
Evil Deities lowered its head and was silent for a while. Suddenly, it looked up and laughed. "Haha, what an unexpected move. You almost defeated me. You're the only person I admire. Unfortunately …"
"Unfortunately what?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"It's a pity that I lost a finger …"
Evil Deities said with an evil smile. He raised his broken index finger and waved it at Luo Zheng. Then, his body collapsed on its own.
In another part of the Chaotic World, an index finger grew rapidly. It grew a complete hand, arm, body, and then a head and limbs.
This was another complete Evil Deities.

Chapter 3902
Just now, when he was chasing Luo Zheng all the way, he felt that something was wrong.
This kind of intuition had always been very accurate, although he was not sure what killing move Luo Zheng would use.
In the process of space shifting, he quietly cut off one of his fingers and threw it in the void. It was this action that saved his life.
"Hum …" The space not far away fluctuated like water.
Luo Zheng slowly stepped out of the fluctuation. He frowned and looked a little gloomy. He did not have much time left.
Evil Deities grinned. "It's not that easy to kill me."
"I just want you to return to me," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
"Why don't you use the move just now on the Descender? The Descender should be able to stop the Wall World from expanding when he loses his energy," Evil Deities replied.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "The Descender doesn't need energy."
He had previously asked Cong for confirmation. Cong told Luo Zheng that the reason why the Descender could not be stopped was because it was the embodiment of the rules and had nothing to do with energy.
"Do you still want to fight?"
Evil Deities asked again.
"I'll keep you company," Luo Zheng said expressionlessly.
The endless energy began to flow again. Now both of them had the energy to stop the flow of energy and tried to throw each other into the energy hole.
But for existences like Evil Deities and Luo Zheng, once they understood the principle, it was impossible for them to fall into the hole. This method was bound to fail.
More importantly, both of them were aware of the threat of the energy hole. They only needed to take advantage of the other's unpreparedness to peel off parts of their bodies and quietly place them in various corners of the chaotic world.
After Evil Deities removed the restraining field, both of them lost the power of omniscience. It was difficult for them to notice these insignificant parts. How could they kill them?
The battle between the two sides did not last too long. After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the Wall World had expanded to the size of a hundred mother worlds.
After the time it took to burn three sticks of incense, the Wall World had expanded to one-tenth the size of the chaotic world.
After the time it took to burn five sticks of incense, the Wall World had occupied one-third of the chaotic world! The Descender moved at an accelerated speed. The speed of expansion was beyond the estimation of Luo Zheng and Evil Deities.
In the immortal abode, Fuxi was already dead drunk. With his cultivation level, it was impossible for him to get drunk. He had purposely put himself in this state.
Nuwa, Hua Tianming, Ku Shu, and the others who had lost their physical bodies were paying attention to the movements in the Chaotic World. The battle between Luo Zheng and the Evil Deities had caused too much noise.
Even though they were separated by more than half of the Chaotic World, they could still feel the remaining power. If it weren't for the protection of the Immortal Mansion and the black ship, the people inside would have died thousands of times.
"Look! What's that over there? "
Luo Zheng suddenly pointed to the west.
Everyone looked in the direction he was pointing and saw an azure arc in their line of sight. This arc was approaching at a very fast speed.
The Giant Wall World itself was absolutely transparent. It was like a piece of colorless ice. Only when something was pressed into the Giant Wall World would it show strange shapes. However, when the three-grid world turned into the Giant Wall World, it would tear and crush the space. That was why people could see the blue arc. It was the light released when the space was broken.
"It's the Giant Wall World! It's so fast!"
Nuwa was surprised.
Luo Zheng had reminded them that the world had started an irreversible degradation, but at that time, the scope of the degradation was very small. They always thought that it would take a while, but they never thought that the speed would be so fast.
"Late or early, it's bound to happen. We can't escape …" Fuxi leaned against the wall and said drunkenly.
Nuwa frowned slightly and reached out to Fuxi. His drunkenness disappeared without a trace. Even if he had reached the point where he couldn't work hard, she didn't want to hear Fuxi's discouraging words.
The azure arc was getting closer and closer to them. Ten million miles, five million miles, one million miles … With the current speed of the arc, it would only take a blink of an eye to cross a million miles.
"Retreat!"
At this time, Luo Zheng's voice suddenly sounded.
The Immortal Mansion began to move.
The Immortal Mansion flew all the way to the east, always keeping a certain distance from the azure arc.
The same was true for the black ship not far from the Immortal Mansion.
Tongtian and the others naturally also saw the rapidly approaching azure arc. The old captain drove the black ship to the east for the first time, but how could the speed of the black ship escape the speed of the degradation?
Seeing that the black ship was about to be overtaken by the Wall World, the Evil Deities also appeared on the deck of the black ship. The black ship's speed immediately increased and began to quickly leave the Wall World … "According to the current speed of the expansion of the Wall World, there is about an incense time left," Luo Zheng said to the crowd.
"That means we can live for an incense time?"
Hua Tianming asked.
Luo Zheng shook his head, "No, I will take you out of the chaotic world to avoid the degradation."
"How long can we avoid it?"
Luo Xiao asked.
Luo Zheng said truthfully after a little calculation in his heart, "Not too long, probably only three or four hours …" Although the Xuanliang world was countless times larger than the chaotic world, the Wall World was expanding at an accelerated speed. No matter how large the world was, it could be swallowed and degraded in a short time.
When the others heard this answer, their hearts slightly tightened, followed by a kind of relief.
Previously, they were worried about how long they could live. Now that they got a definite answer, the last bit of anxiety disappeared.
"Senior Ku Shu will fall earlier," Luo Zheng looked at Ku Shu and said.
After the chaotic world was degraded, the Nirvana Realm would no longer exist. The coordinates of Ku Shu in the Nirvana Realm would also be degraded, and Ku Shu's treacherous body would naturally collapse.
In contrast, Wen Dizhi and Ling Shuang were luckier. They were living people and had no coordinates. They had to rely on the Ring of Descent to descend from the Nirvana Realm. Luo Zheng only needed to provide energy to the Ring of Descent.
Ku Shu looked very calm. He touched Ling Shuang's hair, "Father has to go first."
"Father!"
Ling Shuang's eyes were full of tears.
"Husband! I will follow you right away! "
Yuzhi's face was full of reluctance.
Ku Shu nodded with a smile. There was no sadness on his face. For him, this was not an unacceptable ending.
"Drink up!"
Fuxi took out a wine pot and shook it in front of everyone.
Hua Tianming, who did not touch a drop of wine, suddenly got up. He walked over to Fuxi and took the wine pot. He raised his head and took a big gulp. Then he silently retreated to one side and sat down cross-legged.
Wen Dizhi also walked over and took the wine pot.
Next was Gu Bei, Luo Xiao … Thirty minutes passed.
The Wall World had expanded to the edge of the chaotic world. Immortal Mansion naturally also came to the edge. Luo Zheng raised his hand and blasted a palm at the inner wall of the chaotic world.
"Boom!"
A big hole broke open in the inner wall of the chaotic world. Immortal Mansion directly rushed in through the hole and immediately entered the Xuan Liang World.
Most of the people in Immortal Mansion directly fell into a state of confusion. They only felt that they saw infinite light spots and could not distinguish anything.
Luo Zheng gently patted them, urging them to undergo soul lattice reincarnation. Only then did the Xuan Liang World appear clearly in front of them.

Chapter 3903
The group of people in the chaos successfully integrated into the Xuanliang World, but the situation was different for the creatures from the four-grid world.
Ku Shu's eyes were full of enthusiasm, he gazed deeply at everything here, and the glorious scenes of the Four Spirit Gates appeared in his heart.
He only took a glance, and before he could exclaim, the Treacherous Body began to solidify, and then it began to collapse, turning into dust and dissipating.
Just as Luo Zheng said, after the chaos world was downgraded, the Nirvana World was not spared.
Although the Human Spirits in the Great Guessing Manor hid to the highest point, they only delayed it for a few breaths.
Yuzhi held the Human Spirit King's hand tightly, but in the end, she only caught a handful of dust.
Her eyes dimmed slightly, she gently raised her hand and opened her palm, letting the dust drift away.
According to the rules, Yuzhi, Wen Dizhi, and the others would be obliterated by the power left behind by the downgraded ones when they returned to the Xuanliang World.
Yuzhi and Wen Dizhi could not resist this power, but it was a piece of cake for the current Luo Zheng.
When the obliterating power rushed towards Yuzhi and Wen Dizhi, a faint light film appeared above the Immortal Mansion, easily resisting it. Then Wen Dizhi asked, "Luo Zheng, can you go to the Four Saint Pillars?"
The Four Saint Pillars was once the headquarters of the Four Spirit Gates, Wen Dizhi had dreamed of his hometown countless times, and in his last moments, he had the desire to go home.
At the last moment, Luo Zheng would grant the wishes of the people in the Immortal Mansion, he immediately nodded, "Of course."
"Whoosh …" The Immortal Mansion increased its speed and flew towards the Four Saint Pillars.
The black ship rushed out of the chaos world from the other side.
Although the Evil Deities did not know about the "Soul Lattice Reincarnation", he was a little confused when he saw the stars in the sky when he first entered the main world. But after all, he controlled all the energy and reacted quickly, he figured out the method of Soul Lattice Reincarnation in an instant, and used it on himself, Xiuzhu, Shen Tu, Tongtian, and the old captain.
Han Ge's coordinates were also left in the Nirvana World, although it was hidden very well, the entire Nirvana World was downgraded, how could he be spared?
When Tongtian and the others saw everything in front of them, Han Ge had already turned into ashes.
"Husband, where are we going?"
Xiuzhu asked.
"I don't know." Evil Deities was a little annoyed.
After the entire Chaotic World was downgraded, the Wall World was still expanding at a very high speed. The rocks, woods, mountains, rivers, and sub-worlds were swallowed up and squeezed by the Wall World, turning into a paste like painting … The four-grid world was not small, but it had an end. It was useless to hide anywhere. Evil Deities was annoyed, but they could not think of a solution.
It was meaningless to fight with Luo Zheng. Degenerates couldn't be killed, and the expansion of the Giant Wall World couldn't be reversed. It was a hopeless situation! "No, there must be a way to break it … It's just that I haven't found the right way!"
"…" Evil Deities paced back and forth on the black ship's deck. Suddenly, a cold hand held him. Evil Deities looked up and saw Xiuzhu smiling at him. "Don't worry. I've never seen you like this. You didn't even act like this when facing the creator."
"That's because Luo Zheng still has flaws. He's soft-hearted, so I can always seize the opportunity. But now I can't find any way to break the current situation!"
Evil Deities said with a frown. He was like a caged beast, exuding a restless aura. Shen Tu, the old captain, and Tongtian didn't even dare to speak.
Xiuzhu said gently, "Not every situation can be solved. Maybe you really can't resist the downgrade?"
Evil Deities' mind was spinning rapidly. He was still thinking about various solutions, but when he heard Xiuzhu's words, his eyes widened and his whole body froze.
"I really can't resist …" Evil Deities lowered his head.
"We can accept this situation," Xiuzhu still smiled and comforted him. She didn't want to see Evil Deities so distressed. If there were only a few hours left to live, she hoped that Evil Deities could live happily.
"But I can't!"
Evil Deities' eyes flashed with a murderous aura.
Seeing Evil Deities like this, Xiuzhu bit her lips, lowered her eyes, and sighed softly.
This kind of situation was inevitable, whether they accepted it or not.
The Wall World began to expand rapidly in the Main World. Because the expansion speed was too fast, the creatures living in the Main World could only see a blue arc. They were pressed into the Wall World before they could even scream. They became a mass of flesh and blood that could not be distinguished.
Villages, tribes, and even some sparse cities that were just born could not escape the fate of being swallowed. In two hours, the Wall World had been reduced to one-tenth of the Main World.
The four Holy Pillars were still standing on the ground. Four Chains of Time linked them together, and in the center of the link was the Eternal Divine Court.
"Whoosh …" The huge Immortal Mansion slowly approached the Eternal Divine Court.
Wen Dizhi jumped out of the Immortal Mansion and stood in the center of the courtyard, his face full of excitement! "What a pity, what a pity that I came back alone. My brothers have no chance to see it!"
Wen Dizhi thought of the brothers of the Great Guessing Residence being annihilated in the Wall World, and his heart was filled with sorrow.
"I'm sure they'll be glad to know that you're back," Luo Zheng said.
Wen Dizhi nodded, "Unfortunately, we can't stay here for long."
Luo Zheng looked back into the distance. In the distance, there was a thin blue line that spread like a burning mountain fire.
"We can stay for another hour."
After an hour … the Wall World had been reduced to ninety percent of the Main World.
Immortal Mansion began to rise vertically. Luo Zheng's goal was to retreat into the deep space.
Previously, Luo Nian went to the deep space through the transmission array at the top of the Eternal Divine Court. Luo Zheng naturally did not need to do so. With his ability, he could directly fly up with Immortal Mansion.
After entering the deep space, Luo Zheng once again released the soul lattice reincarnation to the people of Immortal Mansion so that they could adapt to the environment of the fifth grid world.
After reaching the deep space, with Luo Nian's guidance, they flew toward the southwest of Skywheel City and finally stopped in front of a huge stratum.
The arrival of the immortal abode attracted the attention of the Hun tribe below. The Hun tribe members shouted loudly and drew out a white-haired old man. This was Elder Xue.
Elder Xue saw the sudden appearance of Immortal Mansion and had a premonition in his heart.
Soon, several figures flew out from Immortal Mansion. He recognized one of the familiar figures. It was Luo Nian! And he also recognized the other figure! "Mansion, mansion master!"
Elder Xue's voice trembled.
Wen Dizhi strode forward and stretched out his hands to support Elder Xue, saying, "I'm ashamed, I'm ashamed."
In the past, because of the dispute over the route, Wen Dizhi strongly opposed to going to the deep space, but Elder Xue stubbornly proved that his choice was right.

Chapter 3904
When Elder Xue sent Luo Nian away, he was very disappointed.
Unfortunately, he couldn't return to the main world, so he didn't have the chance to meet his old friends.
But there was a hope in his heart that Luo Nian could bring people here. He didn't expect his hope to come true in such a short time.
"We are all loyal to the Four Spirits Sect, but we have different opinions. Master, why should you feel guilty?"
Elder Xue waved his hand.
Wen Dizhi smiled bitterly, "It's not just different opinions. Master Ku Shu and I chose the Bottomless Abyss, which caused the destruction of the Four Spirits Sect. How can I erase the shame in my heart?"
"Don't keep the past in your heart …" Elder Xue said halfway, then looked at the people behind him and asked, "Where is my king?"
Wen Dizhi's expression froze, and he said unnaturally, "Master Ku Shu has died."
"How is that possible!"
Elder Xue's body stiffened slightly, "You …"
Since Luo Nian could bring these people to the deep space, everything should be going smoothly. Ku Shu had a strange body, and except for the coordinates, he had no flaws. It was not easy to kill him.
Wen Dizhi sighed slightly, but didn't know how to answer Elder Xue's question.
"What happened?"
Elder Xue realized that the atmosphere was a little strange.
At this time, Luo Zheng stepped forward and cupped his hands to Elder Xue, "Elder Xue, I am Luo Zheng, Luo Nian is my son."
Although Elder Xue had never seen Luo Zheng, he knew from Luo Nian that the person in front of him was his original plan, and also the existence who had fused with the Origin Divine Blood.
"Elder Xue, you gave me an opportunity and a great plan, but I failed to live up to it," Luo Zheng said, and was about to bow to the ground.
Luo Zheng and Elder Xue met for the first time, and it was this old man in front of him who changed his life.
Ever since that burned page, Luo Zheng's fate had been linked to the entire world. The person he wanted to thank the most was Elder Xue. Unfortunately, Elder Xue's plans had all been for naught.
Elder Xue stretched out his hands and supported Luo Zheng. He said solemnly, "As the saying goes, man proposes, and God disposes. You have inherited the Origin Divine Blood and saved Master Ku Shu. You have also experienced great hardships. You have fulfilled my wish. How can you say that you have failed me?"
"But the world is being destroyed," Luo Zheng said with a bitter smile. He then narrated everything that had happened after Luo Nian had brought back the Skyring Scepter, from the Main Realm to the Chaotic World, from the Descent of Destiny to the Descent of Degeneration. In the end, he said, "I am useless. I did not lead everyone to visit you, Elder Xue. I led everyone to escape here!"
In the process of listening to Luo Zheng's narration, Elder Xue went from being nervous to shocked. In the end, the expression on his old face gradually calmed down, and he finally said with a smile, "Since demotion cannot be prevented, this is the fate of all living beings. Why do you have to take the responsibility on yourself?"
"Yes," Wen Dizhi also nodded, "You didn't lose to the Degrader, but to the law. The law can't be violated, so we can only accept it. We have no other choice …"
While they were talking, not far away, several Xiong people ran over, pointing in a direction and mumbling.
Hearing this, Elder Xue gazed in that direction. A huge blue arc slowly rose from the bottom of the space, eroding all the huge strata, fragments and rocks along the way.
"That … is the expanding Wall World?"
Elder Xue asked.
Luo Zheng gazed, nodded and said, "Yes, the Wall World is about to expand to here. Elder Xue, please come to Immortal Mansion and travel with us."
But Elder Xue shook his head, "You can go. I will stay here."
"Elder Xue, come with us, or you will be demoted," Luo Nian suddenly stood up and said.
Elder Xue looked at Luo Nian and nodded with a smile, "Good boy, we are all human spirits who have lived for countless years. A few more hours, a few less hours, what does it matter?"
Luo Nian scratched his head, "That's true …"
No one tried to persuade Elder Xue. After all, everyone's ending was the same.
Everyone in Immortal Mansion had escaped the shackles of life very early on, but they could not escape the shackles of the world.
The group returned to Immortal Mansion and set off again.
After leaving Immortal Mansion, Elder Xue suddenly spoke to the Xiongnu people.
He told the Xiongnu people that a great sacrifice was about to be held and that all the Xiongnu people should gather.
The Xiongnu people regarded Elder Xue as their spiritual totem. As soon as he spoke, the entire Xiongnu people quickly became busy. Although they did not know what great event was about to descend, the great sacrifice was the most important thing for the Xiongnu people.
After the Xiongnu people had gathered, Elder Xue loudly recited in the Xiongnu language, "The Gate of Heaven is about to descend. It will lead us to the Pure Land of Bliss, where there is no pain or strife, where all living things are equal …"
After hearing this, the Xiongnu people were all delighted. One of them asked, "Where is the Gate of Heaven?"
"There it is," Elder Xue shook his finger, his voice like a great bell roaring out.
The blue arc had already swept over. It was so fast that it almost did not give anyone time to react. The Xiongnu people were basically immersed in the beautiful scenery described by Elder Xue before they were crushed into the Wall World and turned into a blurry mass of flesh and blood.
"Ka ka ka ka …"
After the Wall World expanded into the deep space, its speed increased by another order of magnitude.
After the huge stratum below was swept clean, the Skywheel City began to be degraded. The huge Skywheel City was degraded in just the time it took to burn a stick of incense.
Next, it began to sweep the scattered ruins in the deep space, as well as the ring-shaped sun hanging in the sky.
The shape of the whole deep space was a flattened cone. Even if the person who degraded it did not completely reduce it at the beginning, the deep space world was broken in many places, but the overall shape did not change much.
The Holy Spirit Mansion continued to rise along the central axis of this cone, and finally stopped when it reached the top.
"We can't go any further," Luo Zheng said.
Everyone looked up at the sky. There was a boundary in every world. About seventy or eighty feet above their heads was the boundary of the deep space. It was like a layer of soft gray soil, transparent and opaque at the same time.
After running around for so long, they had finally reached a point of no return.
"Father, what do you think is outside the deep space?"
Luo Nian asked curiously.
"Even 'from' can't answer this question," Luo Zheng replied.
"Why don't we try to break it?"
Luo Nian clenched his fists. He also knew that such an action was futile, but he still wanted to try it out of curiosity.
"We can try …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he saw a black light rising rapidly from below, then it quickly slowed down and finally stopped at about the same height as the Holy Spirit Mansion. It was the black ship controlled by the Evil Deities.
The Evil Deities were not familiar with the route between the Main World and the deep space. They ran blindly all the way, almost being chased by the Wall World, and finally found the top of the deep space.

Chapter 3905
Evil Deities and Luo Zheng glanced at each other after the black ship stopped steadily on one side of Immortal Mansion. Both of them were on guard.
Although both sides had been forced to the point of no return, neither of them intended to take a step back.
"Humph!"
Evil Deities snorted and jumped up from the ship's bow, climbing to the top of the gray soil layer.
His idea was the same as Luo Nian's. Since he couldn't stop the descent, he would break this world and escape.
Luo Zheng calmed down when he saw Evil Deities' move.
He knew that this gray soil layer couldn't be broken. Since Evil Deities made the first move, he would let him have his way.
"Whoosh!"
Evil Deities punched the gray soil layer at the top without hesitation.
"Bang!"
The punch hit the gray soil layer and made a dull sound. The soft gray soil layer only slightly caved in with a fist mark. The energy of the punch was completely absorbed by the gray soil layer.
The Evil God's face was a little surprised. This unremarkable layer of gray soil actually had such a strong endurance.
He retreated a few feet.
His body began to glow, and the energy in the void rushed into his body. He looked like a human-shaped energy body.
When Evil Deities absorbed the energy, Luo Zheng put some distance between himself and Immortal Mansion. When Evil Deities absorbed the energy, there would be a break in the flow of energy. He had to avoid falling into it.
Evil Deities didn't plan to attack Luo Zheng.
After absorbing enough energy, he once again charged toward the layer of gray soil. His entire body directly collided with the layer of gray soil.
"Bang!"
The sound of this collision was much louder than the previous one.
At such a close distance, the gray soil layer caved in again, but it was still only the shape of a head!
Luo Zheng stared at this scene, but his pupils shrank slightly. "This gray soil layer … absorbed the energy?"
The energy in the void would flow back into the void after being used. This was also the law of infinite energy. However, the soil layer had absorbed all the energy, which meant that a part of the energy in the world had disappeared?
Evil Deities had the same expression as Luo Zheng. According to the conclusion drawn by Evil Deities, no matter how many grid worlds there were, the total amount of energy contained was constant and could be accurately calculated down to the tiniest degree.
However, the gray soil layer could absorb energy, which directly overturned the conclusion of Evil Deities.
"It's not what you think. This layer of gray soil can indeed absorb energy, but it will spit it back out after a period of time," Cong's voice sounded in Luo Zheng's mind.
"Could it be that the gray soil layer has no upper limit for absorbing energy?"
Luo Zheng exclaimed.
"Last time, I told you that I used 99% of my energy to launch an energy attack in the eighth grid. It was aimed at this layer of gray soil," Cong replied.
"What was the result?"
Luo Zheng asked hurriedly.
Although Luo Zheng could now control the energy without limit, 99% of the energy in the world was still very shocking.
"It created a large hole with a diameter of more than ten li. I couldn't see where the upper limit of the gray soil layer was. Later, as the gray soil layer slowly calmed down, the energy gradually returned," Cong said calmly.
Hearing Cong's words, Luo Zheng's cheeks twitched twice.
The shockingly huge energy attack only created a large hole more than ten li deep. What exactly was this gray soil layer in front of them?
Evil Deities still didn't give up after the second failure. He moved the black ship some distance away, and said to Luo Zheng in Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion, "You, back off a little!"
He wanted to absorb more energy, but he didn't want Luo Zheng to misunderstand him.
"It's useless," Luo Zheng said.
"You mean this layer of gray soil can't be opened?"
Evil Deities asked.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded, "Cong once used 99% of the energy in the world to create a hole more than ten li deep, but he still couldn't open it."
"…"
Evil Deities clenched his fist, and the surrounding space fluctuated.
No matter what the situation was, he wanted to turn the world upside down, but in the face of this seemingly soft soil layer, he was helpless!
"Bang!"
While feeling distressed, the Evil God's fist smashed into the gray soil again.
Just as he was feeling depressed, Xiu Zhu quietly flew up and hugged the back of the Evil God. She said softly, "You really don't have to worry about it anymore. Use this last bit of time to come back to the ship and accompany me …"
Evil Deities asked, "We're going to die soon! Aren't you afraid of death? "
"I'm not afraid. Dying by your side is not a sad thing," Xiuzhu replied calmly.
Hearing this, Evil Deities' heart trembled slightly, and the annoyance in his heart disappeared.
For the first time, he, who had never been manipulated by fate, compromised from the bottom of his heart. He held Xiuzhu's hand and led her back to the black ship.
Sect Leader Tongtian, Shenshu, and the old captain already knew their fate, and each of them had different expressions on their faces.
The people in Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion began to say goodbye to each other. They all knew that this parting was permanent, but they still remained calm. This excessive calmness revealed a trace of indescribable sadness and reluctance.
In the courtyard of Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion, Luo Zheng's doppelganger said goodbye to Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, Xun, Su Lingyun, and the other girls one by one. Previously, Luo Zheng had told them about the possible consequences. When the moment really came, even Xi Youqin was very strong.
For the next period of time, Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion and the black ship were silent.
In the distance, a blue arc expanded rapidly. Everything along the way, whether it was a huge object or a speck of dust, whether it was flowing energy or solidified matter, was quickly crushed into the Wall World.
Luo Zheng counted silently in his heart.
Ten, nine, eight, seven …
Four, three, two, one.
"Whoosh …"
Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion and the black ship were crushed into countless pieces in an instant.
Because Luo Zheng and the Evil Deities were more powerful, they resisted a little longer in the face of the power of Descent. But in a very short time, the two of them were pushed into the Wall World without any resistance.
"Hahahahaha …"
"The Starry World, the Normal Lower World, and the Extreme Lower World have all completed their Descent!"
"I declare the beginning of the Wall World era!"
Now, the only thing that maintained the "three-grid" form was the black light in the middle and the Descent in the black light.
The black light also began to descend and slowly sank into the center of the Wall World.
The shape of the matter in the three-grid world was very messy when it descended, and it was even more chaotic when it was pressed into the Wall World. Only the black light was different.
After the black light merged into the Wall World, it formed a very regular spherical shape, surrounded by a circle of ripples of the same length. If one looked down from above the Wall World, it would be seen as a delicate black sun symbol.
Of course, no one in the world could look down on the Wall World.
After the black sun symbol shrank in the Wall World, the Descent drilled out of it and slowly crawled on the plane.
The Descent now looked very strange, as if it was a monster put together by many fragments, like the strange carvings of ancient tribal totems.

Chapter 3906
The Downtrodden crawled around for a distance and saw white lines of different lengths.
Even though he was a Downtrodden, it was difficult for him to adapt to the form of a two-grid world. He was a little stunned when he saw these white Downtrodden.
But soon he realized that these white lines were the ice crystals released by Evil Deities.
These ice crystals still had the cold attribute, but after being Downtrodden, more details were revealed. From one ice attribute, five or six different types of energy could be released.

Chapter 3910

The moment Luo Zheng merged with the consciousness of the Evil Deities, a vast memory world appeared in his eyes. All the memories of the Evil Deities were completely revealed in front of him.
From the birth of the Evil Deities in the Inner World to entering the Mother World, making friends with Shentu, becoming a member of the Kurofune, entering the Nirvana Realm and constantly devouring others … The same thing happened after the Evil Deities merged with Luo Zheng's consciousness. In Luo Zheng's vast memory world, everything was also displayed in front of the Evil Deities, from the Eastern Domain to the Mother World.
"If I want to merge with Luo Zheng's consciousness and replace it, I have to absorb all these memories!"
"Luo Zheng is also absorbing my memories!"
"If I'm slow, I'll be replaced by him!"
A strong sense of crisis spread in the consciousness of the Evil Deities. He began to absorb Luo Zheng's memories frantically.
Luo Zheng obviously realized this. If he wanted to take back the Evil Deities, he had to take over the memories of the Evil Deities. So he kept absorbing the memories of the Evil Deities. However, he was slightly stunned when he started to absorb the memories of the Evil Deities.
He had all the memories of the first half of the Evil Deities.
He even had part of the memories after the Evil Deities entered the Mother World, because he had captured the Evil Deities.
After all, the Evil Deities were born in Luo Zheng's Inner World, and Luo Zheng was omniscient about everything in the Inner World.
In order to deal with the Evil Deities, Luo Zheng had read all the memories of the Evil Deities. He was very familiar with most of the memories of the Evil Deities. For Luo Zheng, absorbing the memories of the Evil Deities was like rowing a boat with the current.
Although the Evil Deities absorbed Luo Zheng's memories in an extremely crazy manner, he had never spied on Luo Zheng's complete memories. Even when Luo Zheng and he shared the broken Mother World as their birthplace, the power of omniscience could not be used together.
One was sailing against the current, while the other was sailing with the current. The difference in speed was obvious.
When the Evil Deities absorbed half of Luo Zheng's memories, Luo Zheng had already absorbed all the memories of the Evil Deities.
A sense of loss rose from the bottom of the Evil Deities' consciousness. The memories of the Evil Deities began to disappear. He slowly couldn't remember his past, his lover, and even his own power, until he … "No! I can't lose, I won't … "When the Evil Deities were completely lost, he instinctively released a consciousness that was close to a roar.
His consciousness was finally annihilated.
Luo Zheng took his place.
From now on, he was the Evil Deities, and the Evil Deities were him. Under the influence of the memories of the Evil Deities, Luo Zheng's aura was changed, tainted with a trace of evil. However, the leader was ultimately Luo Zheng himself, not the Evil Deities.
"Are you ready?"
Luo Zheng was in a tunnel.
"Cong" and "Evil" were also devouring each other. Originally, Luo Zheng was a little worried about the outcome of the battle, but when he thought about the process of devouring the Evil Deities, he didn't need to worry about Cong.
"Cong" had spent endless years alone in the Perfect Land. "Cong" had existed for many times longer than "Evil". The memory length of the two was not on the same order of magnitude. It was impossible for "Evil" to devour "Cong".
After a while, Cong responded, "Evil … has returned to me. He and I have become one."
"Congratulations," Luo Zheng congratulated.
"You won too," Cong said.
"Then … do we still need to fuse?"
Luo Zheng asked hesitantly.
If Cong and Luo Zheng devoured each other, Luo Zheng would definitely lose. Luo Zheng's memory length was like a white steed passing through a crack in a flash compared to Cong.
"If I wanted to fuse with you, why would I make the Perfect Land look like this?"
Cong smiled and said.
It regarded Luo Zheng as its solution. Devouring Luo Zheng was meaningless.
"Then what's the next step?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"Wait, just wait," Cong replied.
In the Origin of Space, where there was no light, no space, and no distance, the two consciousnesses fell silent.
Time didn't pass … because time didn't exist here.
It seemed like a moment, but also like a trillion years.
Luo Zheng felt that there was a gap in front of him.
He knew that this was an illusion, because not only did he not have a body, but he didn't even have a soul. He only had a consciousness that couldn't be erased. But how could the consciousness notice this gap?
Amazing! "You feel it?"
Cong's voice followed.
"Yes," Luo Zheng responded. "What is this?"
"The Origin … is splitting," Cong replied.

Chapter 3911
"Cong" had several guesses about the possible changes to the origin.
The origin might not change at all and remain in this state forever, which meant that the whole world would be completely dead, and Cong and Luo Zheng would be trapped in an eternal prison.
Another possibility was that the origin would be rebooted into a perfect place and return to its original 13-grid world. A world with one grid and a 13-grid perfect place had many similarities, so it was not impossible for the two to transform.
Another possibility was that the origin would split. It had long judged that Luo Zheng and Evil Deities did not belong here, so they could cross the most basic rules of the world.
However, these were all inferences. After all, it had never experienced a downgrade.
Now, Cong's judgment was correct. The origin really began to split slowly.
"What will happen next?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"Perhaps... you and I will have a perfect place again," Cong replied.
"A completely separate world?"
"Yes, you will become another me. Congratulations," Cong said.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng suddenly became anxious. "You promised to give me a real world!"
Luo Zheng did not want a perfect place, nor did he want to be the master of the world. He only wanted to return everything that was destroyed in the past, including his lovers, family, and friends.
"It's not difficult," Cong said indifferently. "I give you my memories, and you can create the reality of the past."
After saying that, its consciousness swept towards Luo Zheng, and this consciousness actually took the initiative to merge with Luo Zheng!
The merging of consciousness was initiated by Cong, and Cong voluntarily gave up control, which meant that Luo Zheng directly swallowed a part of Cong's consciousness, including the memories in the consciousness.
"This... why are you doing this..." Luo Zheng was surprised.
"When the origin is restored to a perfect place, I will welcome a new world. I don't need to restore the past. It's best to give you this part of the memory," Cong replied. It only needed to remember roughly what had happened in the past.
Luo Zheng did not say much, and just waited for the consciousness to slowly merge. During the process of merging, Luo Zheng's memory grew explosively.
This part of Cong's memory included the distribution of any energy and matter in the 13-grid world. This was another level of omniscience. One could imagine how vast this memory was.
It was also because of this that everything could be restored from the memory.
After a long time, that part of Cong's consciousness was completely merged with Luo Zheng.
After an unknown amount of time, the origin had completely split into two. Luo Zheng and Cong each occupied one of the two origin points.
After occupying one origin independently, Luo Zheng completely lost contact with Cong.
In this infinitesimally small world, Luo Zheng's consciousness drifted alone. There was no light, no light, and no feeling. This made Luo Zheng feel even more lonely.
Bored to death, Luo Zheng began to read the memories to pass the time.
In this memory, Luo Zheng could find every living being that had ever existed. Among them were kings, traitors, dreamers … The mental journey of these various living beings from birth to death was like a book that was unreservedly displayed in front of Luo Zheng.
After reading through the memories of tens of thousands of living beings, the Origin began to change.
The boundless pressure suddenly disappeared.
At the same time that the pressure disappeared, the matter and energy that had been highly compressed began to rebound wildly, and the infinitely small origin abruptly exploded.
. Boom!
Energy began to flow silently.
A space that could accommodate matter appeared.
The stagnant time began to flow.
Light began to spread.
Sounds that had not been heard for a long time were also heard. Even the noisy explosions sounded like the sounds of nature to Luo Zheng.
A huge space appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Although there was nothing in this space, it did not give people a feeling of emptiness. On the contrary, it seemed very perfect.
It was indeed a perfect place...
. Luo Zheng had seen this place in Cong's memories. It was exactly the same as the scene in front of him.
Everything in this area was eternal. There would be no more pain, parting, or betrayal. Luo Zheng could build the Immortal Mansion here. Everything would be perfect....
. With a thought, he listed the required memories.
. Whoosh..
. Bluestones appeared in the void, forming a tall courtyard wall. The Immortal Mansion was restored in a moment. The people in the Immortal Mansion were also restored.
Behind the high wall came Luo Yunxin's giggles, Luo Nian's strange cries, and Ning Yudie's scolding.
Before he even entered the door, he could already feel the enthusiasm and energy radiating from all directions.
Everything was so perfect.
After Luo Nian transformed into his own body, he walked to the Immortal Mansion. When he came to the door and was about to step in, he suddenly stopped. He could not take this step no matter what.
The Immortal Mansion was indeed that Immortal Mansion. The people were also those people. Everything was perfect in the perfect place.
But this kind of perfection was not pleasing. It even made Luo Zheng subconsciously feel a little disgusted.
This was not the reality he wanted.
"Destroy."
Luo Zheng spat out softly.
The Immortal Mansion disappeared. The people inside also disappeared.
He already understood that what he wanted to create was not just a Immortal Mansion and the people in it. He wanted to return to the previous world.
Although that world was not perfect, there were diseases, separation, death, and fear...
. But that was the real world.
In contrast, everything in the perfect place seemed fake.
"Too small. This place is too small. It has to be downgraded..."
Luo Zheng's voice fell. The 13 spaces in the perfect place opened. After losing some details, it became 12 spaces. The only energy was divided into two.
11 spaces. The energy was divided into four.
10 spaces. The space was divided into eight.
9 spaces..
8 spaces..
7 spaces..
6 spaces..
5 spaces.
Luo Zheng stopped downgrading the world to this time.
Luo Zheng could restore the world to any time. At this level, he controlled time. He had to decide from which point in time to restore the world.
After thinking for a while, he determined the direction. His body appeared at the top of the five-space world.
Above his head was the indestructible layer of gray soil. Immortal Mansion was forced to downgrade here. The people in Immortal Mansion also died after saying goodbye to each other.
"Memory..
"Restore."
Immortal Mansion was created in an instant.
At this moment, the people in Immortal Mansion were about to face death. Although they were calm, they were still full of sadness.
Xi Youqin bit her lip and covered the eyes of Luo Yunxin in her arms. Luo Yunxin did not know her fate and asked her mother what was wrong.
"Nothing, nothing..." Xi Youqin tried not to cry.
"Mom, the blue line is gone," Luo Yunxin said again. Her big eyes looked through the gaps between her fingers.
Xi Youqin had closed her eyes. After hearing Luo Yunxin's words, she opened them again.
The blue arc was indeed gone. In Immortal Mansion, she and Luo Yunxin were intact. The only one who disappeared was Luo Zheng's doppelganger.
"Why?
We're... not dead? "
Xi Youqin murmured.
Ning Yudie, Su Lingyun, Xun and the other girls also asked the same question. They were not dead. It was just that Luo Zheng's doppelganger lying next to them had disappeared.
In the courtyard of Immortal Mansion, Fuxi, Luo Nian, Hua Tianming and the others also closed their eyes and waited for death.
But after waiting for a long time, they were still alive.
"Miracle, a miracle has happened?"
Luo Nian had been praying with his eyes closed. He did not know who could save him. Even his father was helpless. He could only hope for a miracle to turn everything around. And his prayer was successful.

Chapter 3912
Fuxi, Hua Tianming, Wen Dizhi, and the others looked at each other.
They found it hard to believe that they weren't dead.
Nuwa's eyes were full of confusion. She looked into the distance and said in a trembling voice, "The Wall World disappeared?
No, all the matter in the world disappeared? "
The space outside the immortal mansion was empty. Nothing existed … This was obviously not normal.
Downgrade destroyed everything, but the energy and matter in the body would not be damaged at all.
Now even the Wall World was gone! She looked around. "Where's Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng is also gone? "
Hearing this, everyone began to look for Luo Zheng. But where was Luo Zheng's shadow in the immortal mansion?

"He saved the immortal mansion?"

This is a brand new world. I have recreated all of you according to Cong's memories. "
Luo Zheng blinked and looked at his own body, "I'm created from memory … so I'm a fake Luo Nian?"
"Haha …" Luo Zheng was amused by Luo Nian's words, but the others didn't laugh. They obviously had the same worry in their hearts. Luo Zheng had also had this worry before. They were afraid that this was an illusory world.
Luo Zheng could only explain it in detail, "If the energy is arranged according to the original law and condenses into a substance, then they are the original one. This is absolutely true. If you melt a piece of ice and condense it into another piece of ice, is this piece of ice not the original one?"
Nuwa was the first to understand. "You mean that we have indeed died, but you have the memories of the entire world and have reconstructed it according to the memories?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.

"Evil Deities?"
Luo Zheng glanced at Xiuzhu indifferently. "He's dead."
"Ah …" Xiuzhu opened her mouth slightly and looked at Luo Zheng with a trace of sadness in her eyes. "Why aren't we dead …" "You were recreated by me," Luo Zheng said.
For others, it was difficult to accept the reality of Luo Zheng's creation, but Xiuzhu was born in Luo Zheng's inner world. She thought that Luo Zheng was omnipotent, so it was normal for him to recreate her directly.
Hearing this, her eyes lit up slightly. "Then … can you recreate the Evil Deities?"
"No." Luo Zheng refused at once.
The Evil Deities had already comprehended infinite energy. If he rashly created them, it would inevitably cause endless trouble. It was easy to invite the devil in, but difficult to send him away. This guy was really too difficult to deal with as his other self.
Seeing Luo Zheng's attitude, Xiuzhu's eyelids trembled slightly. She pursed her lips and nodded. "I understand. Thank you, Creator …" After saying this, she turned around and was about to leave.
Looking at Xiuzhu's back, Luo Zheng felt an inexplicable emotion in his heart. It was a kind of sad and depressed begging.
This emotion came from him, but it didn't belong to him. After the consciousness of the Evil Deities completely merged with him, it could more or less affect Luo Zheng's emotions.
The Evil Deities were begging him … or was he begging himself?
Luo Zheng shook his head helplessly and smiled bitterly. He called out to Xiuzhu again. "Wait."
Xiuzhu suddenly turned around and stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
"I will recreate him, but not now," Luo Zheng said.
It was not difficult to erase part of the memory of the Evil Deities and completely restrain them.
Hearing this, Xiuzhu knelt in the air and kowtowed three times to Luo Zheng before returning to the black ship.
Then Luo Zheng gently waved his hand and let the black ship follow behind Holy Mansion.

The golden ring-shaped sun in the deep sky was "restored" by Luo Zheng, followed by all kinds of broken tiles floating in the deep sky. The huge broken buildings and the relatively well-preserved Skywheel City were all "restored".
When Holy Mansion went all the way down, the huge strata floating in the void were also restored, and the deep-space races living in them were also reborn.
For these uncivilized barbarians, they didn't know when they fell, nor did they know when they were reborn. They didn't know that there was a demotion, and their peaceful life was not disturbed.
The only one who knew was Elder Xue.
He calmly welcomed death inside the Xiong tribe, and inexplicably welcomed rebirth. When he was puzzled, he saw Holy Mansion arrive. Luo Zheng welcomed him into Holy Mansion with a smile.
"Why?"
Elder Xue wanted an answer.
"Let Holy Mansion Master Wen answer for you," Luo Zheng replied.
Luo Zheng still had a lot of people to restore, and he didn't have time to explain them one by one.
After restoring the deep sky, he took Holy Mansion and the black ship back to the four-grid world.
In the empty world appeared the earth, mountains, rivers … … the four pillars, the Eternal Shrine, as well as numerous shattered sub-worlds … … Not long after, the immortal mansion and the black ship arrived at the entrance of the Primal Chaos World. Chapter 3913
The chaosworld was still empty, with only an immortal residence and a black ship floating within.

Luo Zheng held a grand banquet in the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion. Nearly a thousand people attended.

The Luo family had six new children, three boys and three girls.
Thirty years later.
At the top of the deep space.
Luo Zheng looked at the gray soil layer in front of him with one hand behind his back.
In the past thirty years, he had done all kinds of common knowledge about the gray soil layer, such as digging a hole on it or digging out a piece of the gray soil layer. Cong had done similar actions before, and he could find them in his memory.
People's thirst for knowledge was endless … At first, Luo Zheng didn't understand why Cong wanted to create evil, but now he could.
For Cong, there was no difference between a perfect place and a thoughtless world.
At this level, eternal imprisonment and eternal freedom were the same concept. Everywhere was a prison. The only difference was the size of the prison.
In the past thirty years, Luo Zheng could achieve all the good things he wanted, and he could also achieve all the good things the people around him wanted. But these good things didn't seem so good to Luo Zheng.
He was more willing to spend time on the gray soil layer, trying to find out the slightest mystery from it.
But all his attempts were in vain.

As he stared silently, he suddenly found a black crack at the top of the gray soil layer! Luo Zheng's eyes widened. His figure quickly floated up and came to the front of the black crack.
The gray soil layer was very difficult to destroy. It was impossible for such a crack to appear for no reason. A little dark green liquid seeped out of the black crack.

This evil liquid is the only thing that can penetrate the gray soil layer …" "I was unable to find the boundary of the black soil layer, but I found an underground cave in the soil layer. This underground cave was opened up. They even opened up a secret passage of unknown length."

After the voice disappeared, the dark green evil liquid quickly solidified and turned gray. The black crack quickly closed and disappeared as if it had never appeared.

Energy surged in the air.
